# phainon — Personal Memories

> Verbatim canon dialogue this Heir spoke, was addressed in, or stood on-stage for. Shared scenes are stored in full under `databank/memories/collective/`; this file keeps the personal thread plus a pointer. Original databank mission files were not modified.

---

### Part 1 — `databank/missions/chapter-01-heroic-saga.md`
**Context:** Chapter 1 — Heroic Saga of Flame-Chase (v3.0) > Mission 1 — Silver Chariot, Away to that Blackened Land > Meeting Phainon and Tribbie
**Kind:** collective-pointer
**Id:** `chapter-01-heroic-saga__meeting-phainon-and-tribbie`
**Collective:** `databank/memories/collective/chapter-01-heroic-saga.md`
**Witness:** spoke

*(Cutscene: A young person with white hair came down from above, defeating all monsters. He grabbed your bat and deflected — and broke — Dan Heng's spear.)*

**Dan Heng:** Look out—!
**??? (Phainon):** You've got something... interesting.

---

**Phainon:** I bear no harm. I just want everyone to be safe.
> *Everyone to be safe?* / *Hey, I can understand what he's saying...?*

**Dan Heng:** Everyone to be safe?

**Phainon:** Roaming around a place like the Abyss with weapons in tow is a show of provocation. Those soldiers are minions of Nikador, the Strife Titan. They're not known to stop until all invaders fall under their feet. If you continue brandishing your weapons, you won't only be putting yourselves in the crossfire... Their looming blades will come down on the heads of the innocent.

**Dan Heng:** A civil approach gets your message across all the same.

**Phainon:** It's difficult to tell if you're friend or foe when you appear out of thin air. Even without your weapons, a great power still lurks within you... I could be putting myself in harm's way for all I know. So, care to share the reason for dropping in on us? Hmm? Visitors from above?

**Tribbie:** Phai—non—! Look what you did again! Agh, you even ruined his weapon! This is no way to be treating strangers!

**Phainon:** I was just trying to fix our problem in the safest way possible.

**Tribbie:** How is this safe!? Hello, friendly strangers! Let's all simmer down and cool our jets. We're all humans here. No need for things to get so heated. Snowy's just worried you're baddies from above, but we know you two are nothing of the sort! Oh right, you don't know us yet. We're Tribbie from Janusopolis, and this is... Snowy! Hurry up and apologize to them!

**Phainon:** Alright, if you say so, Tribbie... I apologize for my earlier behavior. I was overly wary because both of you landed in this tumultuous region.

**Dan Heng:** I understand. We're Trailblazers from beyond the sky.
> *You're telling them everything already?*
**Dan Heng:** There is no point concealing anything. They saw everything. If they wanted us dead, they wouldn't be standing around talking to us.

**Phainon:** ...Not from above the sky but beyond it...?
**Tribbie:** Wow, that's something else altogether... You're lucky you met us, then.
**Dan Heng:** What do you mean?
**Phainon:** She means you're lucky because we won't hurt you, but the same can't be said for others.

**Phainon:** ...Let's talk somewhere else. There are still many refugees who haven't been freed in the temple. We're here to rescue and escort them to the holy city, Okhema. They're the reason I had to be absolutely sure you were not here with ill intent. I'm Phainon from Aedes Elysiae. Sorry if I offended you just now.

> *That's an unusual name.* / *Meh, I preferred it when you were being combative.*

**Dan Heng:** ...You sound disappointed. I'm Dan Heng. This is—
> *I'm (Trailblazer).*
> **Phainon:** Friction really does spark friendships, huh? Here, you can have your weapon back.
> *I'm the Galactic Baseballer.*
> **Phainon:** That sounds mighty impressive, but shouldn't you tell me your actual name?
>> *I'm (Trailblazer).*
>> **Phainon:** Friction really does spark friendships, huh?
> *Does no one else think we look like brothers?*
> **Phainon:** Well how about that? Perhaps it's fate! Friction really does spark friendships, huh?

**Tribbie:** And here's your spear. Don't worry, this is entirely on Snowy. I'll make sure he fixes it for you!
**Phainon:** Ahaha... It seems I'll have to treat Chartonus to a couple of drinks again.

**Dan Heng:** We've just arrived and still have many questions. Please lead the way.

---

### Part 2 — `databank/missions/chapter-01-heroic-saga.md`
**Context:** Chapter 1 — Heroic Saga of Flame-Chase (v3.0) > Mission 1 — Silver Chariot, Away to that Blackened Land > Private Discussion

**Id:** `chapter-01-heroic-saga__private-discussion`

**Witness:** on-stage

> *There's more to this Phainon than meets the eye.*

---

### Part 3 — `databank/missions/chapter-01-heroic-saga.md`
**Context:** Chapter 1 — Heroic Saga of Flame-Chase (v3.0) > Mission 1 — Silver Chariot, Away to that Blackened Land > The Temple Dispute — Noldus and Virtus
**Kind:** collective-pointer
**Id:** `chapter-01-heroic-saga__the-temple-dispute-noldus-and-virtus`
**Collective:** `databank/memories/collective/chapter-01-heroic-saga.md`
**Witness:** spoke

**Virtus:** We've lost far too many to Nikador's forces, Mr. Noldus! I only want for everyone to settle down somewhere safe.

**Noldus:** Lose your faith, and you lose everything. Do you seriously think you can seek shelter in Okhema? Nowhere is safe with the end times looming! I've said my piece. Leave, if that's what you want. Everyone else is staying in this temple, where we enjoy the protection of Janus.

**Phainon:** Don't make this decision in haste, sir—
**Noldus:** I appreciate the concern, esteemed Chrysos Heir. But please respect that we have the right to choose our own fate.

---

**Virtus:** I'm sorry you had to see that. Hmm? These two with you are...?
**Phainon:** Travelers from afar. They're also headed for the holy city. If memory serves, you are... Virtus?

**Virtus:** It's an honor you remember me, Phainon. We are priests from Janusopolis. Armies of the Strife Titan destroyed our home, forcing us to flee. A few days ago, I watched another companion fall to those monsters, and I was shattered... I offered a prayer to Okhema, thinking the answer would take time. I didn't expect your arrival to be so swift...

> *Every place has their fair share of troubles.*

**Dan Heng:** The elder spoke of the "end times" and claimed that no place in Amphoreus is safe. What did he mean by that...?

**Virtus:** Excuse me, sir? Everyone knows about the prophecy. How is it that you—

**Phainon:** Virtus, right now we must focus on convincing Mr. Noldus to return. The Abyss is swarming with fierce enemies. My friends, I have a rather bold request. Might I trouble you to fetch Mr. Noldus? Tribbie will join you. She can also answer any questions you may have.

> *Shall we consider our options?*
> **Dan Heng:** Phainon interrupted me as I was trying to gather information from that refugee, and now he's saying the girl will answer our questions. His intent is obvious.
>> *He's hiding something from the refugees?*
> **Dan Heng:** Yes. I believe he thinks it's worse for others to find out we know nothing about this world. He wants to hide the fact that we're visitors from beyond the sky. There's a rift between us and the locals, so let's play along for now. We can also learn what Tribbie thinks when we're alone with her.

> *We'll take it from here.*
**Dan Heng:** Alright, we'll go.

**Phainon:** You two are truly honorable! I'll stay behind to guard Virtus and the rest. Go quickly and come back safe.

---

---

### Part 4 — `databank/missions/chapter-01-heroic-saga.md`
**Context:** Chapter 1 — Heroic Saga of Flame-Chase (v3.0) > Mission 1 — Silver Chariot, Away to that Blackened Land > The Ballad of the Twelve Titans
**Kind:** collective-pointer
**Id:** `chapter-01-heroic-saga__the-ballad-of-the-twelve-titans`
**Collective:** `databank/memories/collective/chapter-01-heroic-saga.md`
**Witness:** spoke

**Phainon:** The dromas caravan isn't here yet, so let's all rest.

**Dan Heng:** Regrettably, our questions are still unanswered. Now that things have calmed down, perhaps you two can find the time to answer a few?
**Phainon:** Of course. Please come this way.

---

**Phainon:** Please ask me anything you wish to know.

> *(Ask)* What is this "end times"?
> **Phainon:** Just as the word implies, it's the time of strife before the world meets its end.
> **Dan Heng:** Are you saying your civilization is coming to an end?
> **Phainon:** To be precise, it's the whole world that's coming to an end.

> *(Ask)* What are the Titans?
> **Phainon:** The ancient gods of Amphoreus, once worshiped by humanity, now stand as their foes.

> *(Ask)* Who are the Chrysos Heirs?
> **Phainon:** The prophetic Deliverers, with golden blood coursing through their veins.

> *(Ask)* What is the miracle performed earlier?
> **Phainon:** It's a gift from the Three Titans of Fate. With their blessings, priests can awaken shadows of the past to alter reality.
> **Tribbie:** You're putting it too lightly. Don't make miracles sound like a tool you can simply use at will.

> *That's all the questions I have for now.*

**Dan Heng:** Your answers are too concise...
**Phainon:** I'm sorry. I guess I'm not good at telling stories.

**Tribbie:** Yet, a tedious and overly long explanation isn't ideal either. Let me think... Right! Virtus and the others are priests from Janusopolis. They should be adept at playing the lyre. How about playing a song for our two friends? There's a ballad that's been passed down in Amphoreus since ancient times...

**Tribbie:** "Happiness abound in this lush land by gods chosen. Giants raised their glasses in a toast beheld by the twelve constellations."
**Tribbie:** "Three carved the heavens and the earth, three wove the threads of fate. Three molded life with their hands, and three guided calamity's gate."
**Tribbie:** "They declared the world too unstirring, hence wished endless laughter upon the living."
**Tribbie:** "Thus you and I were created, with words and song to weave, and love and intimacy to give."
**Tribbie:** "Thus, creation was complete, who then will bear the weight of humanity's souls?"
**Tribbie:** "O great Kephale, all-knowing father of stature so tall, yet low does their gaze willingly fall."
**Tribbie:** "With light of dawn heavy upon their shoulders, golden ichor spills unto the land..."
**Tribbie:** "...forming the boiling river that flows through the legacies of heroes who take their stand..."

**Phainon:** ...Hmm?
> *What's that sound!?*
**Phainon:** Hahaha! Don't worry. That's just our rescue. Time to get up, everyone!

---

---

### Part 5 — `databank/missions/chapter-01-heroic-saga.md`
**Context:** Chapter 1 — Heroic Saga of Flame-Chase (v3.0) > Mission 1 — Silver Chariot, Away to that Blackened Land > Ride the Dromas to Okhema
**Kind:** collective-pointer
**Id:** `chapter-01-heroic-saga__ride-the-dromas-to-okhema`
**Collective:** `databank/memories/collective/chapter-01-heroic-saga.md`
**Witness:** spoke

**Dan Heng:** What magnificent creatures.
**Phainon:** These large mild-mannered creatures are called dromas. They're humanity's most loyal companions. Dromas are very docile, even first-time riders can steer them easily. Here, give it a try.

**Dromas:** Urrhh... uuuhhhh—

> *Hop on the dromas.*
> *(You see the white shells jutting from the dromas' limbs and plan to use them as footholds...)*
> **Dromas:** Urrhh! *(tosses you several meters away)*
>> *This is your idea of docile!?*
> **Phainon:** I forgot to mention. Don't touch their white stone humps. When the Earth Titan blessed these creatures, it's said they also stuffed their ill tempers into these humps.

> *Carefully ride the dromas.*
**Phainon:** See? Easy, wasn't it?

**Noldus:** Everyone, how did you find our lyre performance? I fear our skills have grown rusty...
**Tribbie:** No, not at all! Your performance was wonderful, sir!

**Noldus:** It's been a long time since we sat together and sang ancient ballads to our heart's content. Looking back now, I admit I was too stubborn. Perhaps you really can save Amphoreus from its doom, just like how the four of you heroes saved our very lives and faith today.

> *He even included the two of us!*

**Phainon:** Suffering's bad for your health, and it won't help your faith much, either. That's why it's our inherent duty as Chrysos Heirs to free everyone from suffering. It's also the reason for the holy city Okhema's existence — the only human city left in our present day.

**Tribbie:** Hey now, don't be so serious. Anyway, in short, the holy city is absolutely safe. Everything will be fine once we get there. Is everyone properly seated? Then we're off! Next stop: Okhema!

---

### Part 6 — `databank/missions/chapter-01-heroic-saga.md`
**Context:** Chapter 1 — Heroic Saga of Flame-Chase (v3.0) > Mission 1 — Silver Chariot, Away to that Blackened Land > Okhema Under Attack!
**Kind:** collective-pointer
**Id:** `chapter-01-heroic-saga__okhema-under-attack`
**Collective:** `databank/memories/collective/chapter-01-heroic-saga.md`
**Witness:** spoke

> **Dan Heng:** They're just minions. My cloudhymn magic will be sufficient.
> *He thought we were Chrysos Heirs.*
> **Dan Heng:** "The Deliverers mentioned the prophecy"... It seems the people have a lot of faith in Phainon and the others. Our priority now is to find Phainon.

---

**Phainon:** The main road's been blocked off! Tribbie...
**Tribbie:** Don't panic. Leave this to us! "I summon thee, Oronyx!"
> *"Lift the curtain of memory—"* / *"—and stir up ripples of past reverie!"*
**Tribbie:** "...Lift the curtain and stir up ripples of past reverie!"

---

**Dan Heng:** How's the situation?
**Phainon:** I never expected Nikador to lead an army and launch a surprise attack on the holy city... They and Kephale have always been mortal enemies, but now they've gone mad like a vicious beast.

> *We took care of some enemies.*
> **Phainon:** You have my gratitude. Thankfully, Okhema is not completely unprepared. The prophecy had warned us about this.

**Tribbie:** Mr. Noldus and the others have gone to seek shelter. You should evacuate along with the citizens.
> *We can help.* / *It's safer by your side.*
**Dan Heng:** What (he/she) meant to say is: We can help.

**Phainon:** Very well, but please remain by my side. Their attacks do not discriminate between friend and foe.

---

---

### Part 7 — `databank/missions/chapter-01-heroic-saga.md`
**Context:** Chapter 1 — Heroic Saga of Flame-Chase (v3.0) > Mission 1 — Silver Chariot, Away to that Blackened Land > Aid Okhema
**Kind:** collective-pointer
**Id:** `chapter-01-heroic-saga__aid-okhema`
**Collective:** `databank/memories/collective/chapter-01-heroic-saga.md`
**Witness:** spoke

**Stubborn Citizen:** No, no, no, it's very safe here. Watch! No one will die. The heroes will protect the city!
**Pious Citizen:** The prophecy has already revealed that the light of Kephale will shine upon all...

**Phainon:** Leave them be. Just as Mr. Noldus reveres Janus, faith is all some people have left in a world teetering on the brink of collapse. To honor their faith is the sacred duty of the Chrysos Heirs— Minions of Strife, you shall not harm these people!

*(Battle against Furiae enemies)*

---

---

### Part 8 — `databank/missions/chapter-01-heroic-saga.md`
**Context:** Chapter 1 — Heroic Saga of Flame-Chase (v3.0) > Mission 1 — Silver Chariot, Away to that Blackened Land > The Three Tribbies
**Kind:** collective-pointer
**Id:** `chapter-01-heroic-saga__the-three-tribbies`
**Collective:** `databank/memories/collective/chapter-01-heroic-saga.md`
**Witness:** spoke

**Phainon:** Bodies everywhere. How could so many enemies have made it to the front gates? Where were the city guards?

**Trianne:** Snowy...
**Trinnon:** We're back.

> *Th—Three Tribbies!?* / *Is this a trick!?*

**Trianne:** We are Trianne!
**Trinnon:** We are Trinnon!
**Tribbie:** We are Tribbie— Wait, no, now is not the time for introductions!

**Dan Heng:** ...The surprises never cease in Amphoreus.

**Trianne:** Snowy, De has been trading blows with the main forces for what feels like forever! Hurry up and assist him!

**Phainon:** I knew it... He gets reckless once he gets the urge to kill. I'm relying on you three to handle getting the citizens to safety.

**Trianne:** Don't worry! With us here, we can just use the Century Gate to whoosh the people to the safest place—
**Trinnon:** Wouldn't that be Okhema?
**Trianne:** ...
**Tribbie:** Stop messing around and help the people! Anywhere is fine!

**Phainon:** Although they are a little childish, the trio are experienced Chrysos Heirs and one of the pillars of Okhema. They can definitely be trusted. Well, let's keep on moving then.

---

---

### Part 9 — `databank/missions/chapter-01-heroic-saga.md`
**Context:** Chapter 1 — Heroic Saga of Flame-Chase (v3.0) > Mission 1 — Silver Chariot, Away to that Blackened Land > Mydei — The Successor of Kremnos
**Kind:** collective-pointer
**Id:** `chapter-01-heroic-saga__mydei-the-successor-of-kremnos`
**Collective:** `databank/memories/collective/chapter-01-heroic-saga.md`
**Witness:** spoke

**Startled Citizen:** L—Lord Phainon! Quick, save the Grand Craftsman! He's surrounded by enemies—
**Phainon:** What!? Chartonus! Hang in there!

*(Cutscene: A blonde man emerged from the gate, dragging the enemy he had defeated.)*

**Mydei:** "The rear is secured now"... This is the result of your efforts?
**Phainon:** Quit messin' around, you know who we're dealing with.
**Mydei:** Carelessness will be your downfall, "Deliverer"!

*(Battle)*
**Mydei:** Mere child's play. A true fight between us would shatter them with the aftershocks alone.
**(Trailblazer):** Are you talking to me?
**Mydei:** Who are you?
**Phainon:** Enough, Mydei. Are you trying to destroy the holy city?

*(After battle)*
**Phainon:** Chartonus!
**Chartonus:** Phainon...
**Phainon:** I'm glad that you're okay... As for you, Mydei... shouldn't your responsibility be protecting the citizens?

**Mydei:** What is that supposed to mean? Who are you implying isn't a citizen here?

**Phainon:** Don't evade the question. Aglaea told us to protect the citizens.

**Mydei:** It is you who forget the second half of her words: "Protect the citizens and eliminate the invaders." Since you've never considered the pair over there to be "citizens"... They seem to fit the definition of "invaders."

**Dan Heng:** What do you mean?
**Mydei:** The three priests of Janus share the same appearance and mind, and their senses are interlinked. The Okhemans have long been aware of your presence. You trust him that much? I suggest you think twice.

**Phainon:** Provoking your allies at such a critical juncture. Whose side are you on?

**Mydei:** From the moment we first met, I told you — the people of Kremnos will never agree to make peace with the likes of you... As the successor of Kremnos, I, Mydei, am not able to act independently. Furthermore, I must warn you, esteemed guests — your host is anything but proficient in the ways of hospitality.

**Chartonus:** Enough... The time for internal strife, now is not. Still on the offense, the mad king is and must be stopped by the Chrysos Heirs...

**Mydei:** I have not forgotten. Stand down. I'm more than capable of taking care of what's ahead by myself. As for you... Nikador is in the Marmoreal Palace. Aglaea told me to stay out of it. So, go ahead... "Deliverer".

**Phainon:** I don't need you to tell me that.

**Mydei:** You, what's your name?
> *My name is (Trailblazer).*
> **Mydei:** Is that not also strange?
> *I am the Galactic Baseballer!*
> **Mydei:** Is that not also strange?
> *Heh, it's a secret!*
> **Mydei:** Isn't that name... also strange?

**Dan Heng:** Is he your companion? You guys don't seem to be on friendly terms.
**Phainon:** Well, that's the Chrysos Heirs. Revered as heroes by the masses, but still imperfect mortals at their core.

---

---

### Part 10 — `databank/missions/chapter-01-heroic-saga.md`
**Context:** Chapter 1 — Heroic Saga of Flame-Chase (v3.0) > Mission 1 — Silver Chariot, Away to that Blackened Land > Castorice — The Shadow of Death
**Kind:** collective-pointer
**Id:** `chapter-01-heroic-saga__castorice-the-shadow-of-death`
**Collective:** `databank/memories/collective/chapter-01-heroic-saga.md`
**Witness:** spoke

**Phainon:** ...What a mess. This is Marmoreal Market, the most important residential area. Pushing through by force would result in needless casualties... But now our backup is here.

**??? (Castorice):** Lord Phainon. And... the two guests. Welcome to Okhema.
**Dan Heng:** These enemies... aren't moving?
**Phainon:** When I heard your footsteps, I thought Death was already writing my final chapter... Miss Castorice.

**Castorice:** An abrupt ending to an epic that has barely begun might also amaze the people. But today, Lord Phainon... Okhema needs you.
**Phainon:** Well, if the "mortician" of the holy city says I'm needed, then it sounds like I'm gonna live through this mess.

**Castorice:** All I can do is clear the obstacles and guide you to the place of Nikador's descent. Dear guests, if you would, please follow me... And kindly keep five paces distance.

---

### Part 11 — `databank/missions/chapter-01-heroic-saga.md`
**Context:** Chapter 1 — Heroic Saga of Flame-Chase (v3.0) > Mission 1 — Silver Chariot, Away to that Blackened Land > Follow Castorice
**Kind:** collective-pointer
**Id:** `chapter-01-heroic-saga__follow-castorice`
**Collective:** `databank/memories/collective/chapter-01-heroic-saga.md`
**Witness:** spoke

**Castorice:** ...No, I'm nobody's attendant.
**Soldier of Nikador:** You can try to hide it, but you obviously came from the world of the dead. Okhema, Chrysos Heirs — what cowards. We live by loyalty and glory, while you live by weakness and deceit.

**Phainon:** I'm so glad that you're not my history teacher. How much can we trust the words of someone who has sunk so deep into madness? However, if you're standing here out of loyalty and glory, then I shall draw my blade and grant you a warrior's end.

**Soldier of Nikador:** Come, to arms!

*(Battle)*

*(After the battle)*
**Phainon:** As you wish.
**Castorice:** Lady Aglaea once said that your time is extremely valuable and shouldn't be wasted on unnecessary conflict. I have the power to lay them to rest.
**Phainon:** It's pointless to waste your strength on these pawns. Let's go. It's time to face "Strife".

**Castorice:** Once there... You must turn into birds and fly toward the final battlefield.
**Phainon:** Miss Castorice, why aren't you joining us?
**Castorice:** I must stay here and ensure that these soldiers do not destroy the city. Although faith is more important to the vast majority, there are still quite a number of citizens who care more about their worldly possessions. The citizens have been evacuated and the only living things in the vicinity... are the three of you. As Lady Aglaea instructed, no civilians will be affected.

**Phainon:** ...Do excuse my impropriety. Well then, I'll see you later, Miss Castorice.
**Castorice:** Then... Could you all quietly leave?

---

### Part 12 — `databank/missions/chapter-01-heroic-saga.md`
**Context:** Chapter 1 — Heroic Saga of Flame-Chase (v3.0) > Mission 1 — Silver Chariot, Away to that Blackened Land > Face Nikador
**Kind:** collective-pointer
**Id:** `chapter-01-heroic-saga__face-nikador`
**Collective:** `databank/memories/collective/chapter-01-heroic-saga.md`
**Witness:** spoke

**Phainon:** Marmoreal Palace, the Lance of Fury, Nikador... They're all there, in the distance.

> *What's that sound!?* / *Mommy, I'm scared...*
**Dan Heng:** What a horrifying battle cry. Is that a Titan?

**Phainon:** That battle cry once crushed all in its path. It knocked down foes and allies alike. My limbs trembled uncontrollably as my weapon clattered to the ground. I could hear nothing but the frenzied and shameful beating of my own heart... Fear — this is why the Strife Titan is revered. If a warrior can face this terror and still step forward, no trial thereafter can shake the weapon in their hand. You two, if you wish to retreat, this is your final chance.

> *It's fine. I've already died a number of times before.*

**Dan Heng:** We have no interest in the Titan rites. As Trailblazers, lending a helping hand to unfamiliar worlds is simply our duty.

**Phainon:** Trailblazing... What an interesting concept. Surely, on your worlds, there must also be a Titan which is revered by the masses? There's no need to further confirm your resolve. We shall soon cross swords with the embodiment of Strife. And yet, faced against this menacing threat, your resolve only gleams more intensely. Let's get going. In the era when Kephale had not yet fallen silent, none could stand as their equal. Today, we inherit the duty of the gods, safeguarding the inhabitants of Amphoreus.

**Phainon:** Stand with me and become heroes!

---

---

### Part 13 — `databank/missions/chapter-01-heroic-saga.md`
**Context:** Chapter 1 — Heroic Saga of Flame-Chase (v3.0) > Mission 1 — Silver Chariot, Away to that Blackened Land > Battle Against Nikador
**Kind:** collective-pointer
**Id:** `chapter-01-heroic-saga__battle-against-nikador`
**Collective:** `databank/memories/collective/chapter-01-heroic-saga.md`
**Witness:** spoke

*(After the Hidden Passage)*
**Dan Heng:** It's deathly silent...
**Phainon:** Deathly silence is the proud resonance of Nikador's war drums. Behind this watery veil... awaits the arena of conflict.

*(After passing first veil)*
**(Trailblazer):** Who's wailing? My head feels as though it's being ripped apart...
**Phainon:** Stay strong! Recall your convictions and everything you hold dear... If you can't do that, then think of death, for doing so might actually keep you alive!

*(Cutscene: In the empty baths, Nikador lay in wait. Delighted by the arrival of challengers, they assumed a combat stance.)*

*(Battle: Savage God, Mad King, Incarnation of Strife)*

**Phainon:** Careful! They look different from what I remember.
**Dan Heng:** How so?
**Phainon:** Weaker... but also more distorted.

**Phainon:** Cast aside all distracting thoughts— To me! I shall withstand their wrath!

*(Titanic Corpus destroyed)*
**Phainon:** Even gods can bleed... I shall extinguish your Coreflame, Titan!

*(Battle ends when Nikador is reduced to 1% HP)*

---

---

### Part 14 — `databank/missions/chapter-01-heroic-saga.md`
**Context:** Chapter 1 — Heroic Saga of Flame-Chase (v3.0) > Mission 1 — Silver Chariot, Away to that Blackened Land > Aglaea — The Goldweaver
**Kind:** collective-pointer
**Id:** `chapter-01-heroic-saga__aglaea-the-goldweaver`
**Collective:** `databank/memories/collective/chapter-01-heroic-saga.md`
**Witness:** spoke

**Aglaea:** Curious about my eyesight? Well, I'm not blind. On the contrary, I can see much more than the typical person. Those with golden blood always have something extraordinary about them. For me, it's perception. I don't need light to measure the world — the wind carries messages along golden threads, delivering countless sensations to my fingertips. Just like how at this present moment, the virtues possessed by the both of you have turned into a warm current that pleases my skin.

> *She sounds so cultured...*
> **Dan Heng:** Indeed, her manner of speaking is comparable to Sunday's.

**Phainon:** A manifestation of Nikador... Could it be that my trial has not arrived yet?

**Aglaea:** By following the threads of fate, you have begun to write the opening chapter of your story. How do you feel?

**Phainon:** Truth be told, I don't feel any different. When Trianne followed those escaped soldiers, was that part of the plan too?

**Aglaea:** Naturally. We have long foreseen this attack and didn't intend to waste such a golden opportunity. After Nikador succumbed to madness, their fortress disappeared into the fog. However, now that they have acted out of character and attacked Okhema... The holy city shall also uncover their hiding place and sound the horn of retaliation.

**Phainon:** What an intricate plan indeed. I gave my word that after the situation stabilized, I would reveal all about Amphoreus to our allies. However, given that Okhema has just barely survived a calamity... Aglaea, would you mind taking care of this in my stead?

**Aglaea:** Distinguished guests, since you have spared no effort in aiding the holy city, I will naturally take care of you as well. I've woven many unforeseen events into the tapestry of fate, this is but one instance.

**Phainon:** You two can find me at Marmoreal Market after you've grown tired of listening to her tales. In any case, I owe you my hospitality.

**Aglaea:** Now then, where should we begin?

---

### Part 15 — `databank/missions/chapter-01-heroic-saga.md`
**Context:** Chapter 1 — Heroic Saga of Flame-Chase (v3.0) > Mission 2 — Distant Travelers, Listen to this World's Prayer > Continue to Explore Okhema
**Kind:** collective-pointer
**Id:** `chapter-01-heroic-saga__continue-to-explore-okhema`
**Collective:** `databank/memories/collective/chapter-01-heroic-saga.md`
**Witness:** spoke

**Chartonus:** ...Out of the shadow of the past, you've not stepped, Chrysos Heir.
**Chartonus:** The weight of the Coreflame, if you wish to bear... You must bury the past, suspend the pain.
**Phainon:** I'll try my best, Chartonus. But why keep mentioning it if you already know I can't do it?
**Chartonus:** ...Here, your guests are.
**Phainon:** (Trailblazer), Dan Heng! You're back much quicker than I imagined. I thought that Aglaea would keep you for a bit longer.
**Phainon:** Here you go. This is the compensation that I previously mentioned. I asked the most famous Grand Craftsman in the holy city to repair your spear.
**Phainon:** Once again, let me offer my sincere apologies for any offense I caused during our first meeting.
**Dan Heng:** ...
**Dan Heng:** Judging solely from its exterior, I cannot see any signs of damage. It's truly incredible.
**Phainon:** The one who crafted this spear must also be a renowned master. I guess there's always someone greater out there. Although I still can't imagine a world outside Amphoreus.
**Phainon:** ...Or perhaps I've just never given it much thought until I met you two.
**Phainon:** You've probably now heard about the tripartite prophecy that descended upon Amphoreus and the mission of the Chrysos Heirs. For us, pursuing the prophecy is the purpose of our journey. Therefore, thinking too much about other things is unnecessary.

> *(Trailblazer)* Hasn't anyone questioned the prophecy?
> *(Trailblazer)* And who was is exactly that issued the prophecy?
> **Phainon:** It's considered a prophecy because it manifested as a miracle, though its origin or whose will it represents remains unknown.
> **Phainon:** There's another theory that it is the whispers of Kephale before their eternal slumber. It was the sky father who scattered their divine blood all over the land that gave rise to the Chrysos Heirs... Of course, this is just one of many popular folktales.
> **Phainon:** Please don't simply dismiss it as silly superstition. The people of this world have already experienced the transition from skepticism to firm belief, and this transition wasn't easy to achieve. Many have paid the price.

**Dan Heng:** What Aglaea conveyed to us was more of a collective mission. As an individual, what role do you play in all of this, Phainon?
**Phainon:** Me? I'm still seeking that answer myself.
**Phainon:** But if the gods favor me, I believe that my Coreflame won't be far.

> *(Trailblazer)* Is it Nikador?
> *(Trailblazer)* Your Coreflame?
> **Phainon:** It's Nikador.
> **Phainon:** I'm a soldier who was taught how to fight at a young age. From the day I was selected by the prophecy, I've regarded the Lance of Fury as my goal.
> **Phainon:** One day, I'll break the lance and take the Coreflame of Strife.
> **Phainon:** ...But discussing such a bitter topic is a bummer. I'm embarrassed since I think I'm the one who brought it up.

**Phainon:** Oh right, Tribbie mentioned that you two also use teleslates. Why don't we exchange contacts? Please also accept these gifts as a token of my appreciation.
**Phainon:** Since the weather is great, why don't you two look around some more? I won't take up more of your time. See you later.

**Context:** Chapter 1 — Heroic Saga of Flame-Chase (v3.0) > Mission 3 — Night Stars, Accompany My Slumber > Submit to Aglaea's Interrogation — It's Better to Speak the Truth

**Castorice:** Please... relax.
*(narrative)* Castorice places her slender, pale finger over your pulse.
*(narrative)* A chill seeps through your skin, then immediately spreads into your veins.
*(narrative)* Castorice adds a second finger... then a third, until half her palm gently envelops your left wrist.
*(narrative)* A subtle fragrance of an unnamed flower fills your senses, its sweetness making you forget the need to breathe.
*(narrative)* Your vision blurs, and cold reality fades into a purple ocean — her promised land of flowers.
*(narrative)* ...
*(narrative)* Then, you finally understand...
*(narrative)* ...you are currently welcoming your own sweet demise.
*(narrative)* ...

**???: ** ...Wait! This story can't end just like that.
**Aglaea:** ...Phainon?

> *(Trailblazer)* I'm still alive.
> **Phainon:** You bet you are.
> *(Trailblazer)* I've been reborn on Amphoreus?
> **Phainon:** Not quite, Friend. You're still alive and kicking.

**Phainon:** You've been on Amphoreus for such a short time. Even someone as ruthless as Thanatos wouldn't end a journey so hurriedly.
**Phainon:** Aglaea, as the first local to encounter them... I have some thoughts I would like to share regarding their fates.
**Aglaea:** Do speak your mind.
**Phainon:** When I first met them in the Abyss, I was wary of them as well and never let my guard down throughout our journey back to the holy city.
**Phainon:** However, during the battle to defend Okhema, we fought side by side numerous times. The look in someone's eyes in moments of life-and-death never lies, and I was able to detect the conviction in their eyes back then. That's why I was willing to put my life in their hands.
**Phainon:** Please believe a warrior's intuition.
**Phainon:** At present, Okhema is desperate for aid, and I am loath to lose two allies just like that.
**Phainon:** Isn't this what Miss Castorice believes as well?
**Castorice:** ...Yeah.
**Castorice:** I'm more willing to view "death" as a peaceful farewell than a punishment.
**Aglaea:** ...I see.
**Aglaea:** So, two Chrysos Heirs are willing to vouch for you. Hah... It seems my actions were too high-handed.
**Aglaea:** Phainon, will you fulfill your duty as host and look after our distinguished guests?
**Phainon:** While I'm not good at formalities, I'll do my best.
**Aglaea:** Cas... Next time, I hope you will express your thoughts openly.
**Castorice:** I will.
**Aglaea:** Now, it's time to welcome the newcomers to the Vortex of Genesis. As you both have chosen to trust these outlanders, ensure they are adequately informed about the mysteries concealed within.
**Dan Heng:** Hold on, you seriously still expect us to help you after what we just went through?
**Dan Heng:** Forgive us, but I'm afraid our cooperation cannot continue under these conditions. If we cannot gain people's — no, if we cannot gain your trust, Lady Aglaea — we'll depart. Amphoreus isn't the only option for the Nameless.
**Dan Heng:** Things don't have to end on a sour note between us.
**Aglaea:** Regardless of your choice, the Chrysos Heirs will continue to follow the path in the prophecy.
**Aglaea:** Phainon's words have convinced me to make an exception for you. Whether you stay or go after this is for you to decide.

**Aglaea:** You have earned a second chance with your honesty.
**Aglaea:** Both of you may continue to stay in Okhema, and its citizens will continue to view you as distinguished guests. However, you must promise that today's farce will not occur again.
**Dan Heng:** I think it's more appropriate for us to say that last part.
**Dan Heng:** You seriously still expect us to help you after what we just went through?
**Aglaea:** Regardless of your choice, the Chrysos Heirs will continue to follow the path in the prophecy.
**Aglaea:** You may be a ray of hope that fell onto Amphoreus. However, until all has been proven, you cannot be allowed to pose a threat to Amphoreus.

> *(Trailblazer)* Are all Chrysos Heirs as paranoid as you?
> **Aglaea:** For the holy city, I am willing to bear all the traits that others despise and use every heartless yet effective trick in the book.

**Dan Heng:** Forgive us, but I'm afraid our cooperation cannot continue under these conditions.
**Dan Heng:** If we cannot gain people's — no, if we cannot gain your trust, Lady Aglaea — we'll depart. Amphoreus isn't the only option for the Nameless.

### Part 19 — `databank/missions/chapter-01-heroic-saga.md`
**Context:** Chapter 1 — Heroic Saga of Flame-Chase (v3.0) > Mission 3 — Night Stars, Accompany My Slumber > Listen to What Phainon Has to Say
**Kind:** collective-pointer
**Id:** `chapter-01-heroic-saga__listen-to-what-phainon-has-to-say`
**Collective:** `databank/memories/collective/chapter-01-heroic-saga.md`
**Witness:** spoke

*(You found out Phainon came because Castorice asked him to stop Aglaea. At the Vortex of Genesis, you saw six Coreflames that had already been lit. When Chrysos Heirs return Coreflames, they become demigods, taking over the Titans' duties and filling their vacant seats. Aglaea and Tribbie were the only two demigods in Okhema and had started the Flame-Chase Journey again. After explaining all this, Phainon suggested listening to the Voice of Constellation.)*

**Phainon:** ...I swear by the honor of the Chrysos Heirs here that your lives were never in danger. Before the interrogation started, Miss Castorice already sent a message to me, hoping that I would stop Aglaea.
**Castorice:** Only a select few can sway Lady Aglaea, and Lord Phainon is one of them.

> *(Trailblazer)* But she didn't even apologize...
> *(Trailblazer)* Phainon's a big deal, huh?
> **Phainon:** Oh, please stop.
> **Phainon:** Aglaea... She has long sealed her heart in ice, for Okhema's sake.
> **Phainon:** Maybe this is the price of being a demigod? Standing among mortals, yet still so far from them.

> *(Trailblazer)* Demigod?
> **Phainon:** Ah... one more thing to explain. Let's not worry about that for now. You'll soon find out that Amphoreus's deepest secrets are all hidden here.
> **Phainon:** Well, first, kindly look upward. What you see right now is the Astral Zodiac of the Vortex of Genesis, divided into twelve parts that record the Flame-Chase Journey of the Chrysos Heirs.
> **Phainon:** The twelve vacant signs symbolize the twelve Titans of Amphoreus, while each illuminated constellation represents one Coreflame that mankind has restored to its rightful place.
> **Dan Heng:** There are... six restored Coreflames. You're already halfway through your journey?
> **Phainon:** The generations before us paid a heavy price for that. The journey of the Chrysos Heirs once faced immense hurdles and made no progress for a very long time...
> **Phainon:** It was Aglaea and Tribbie who rallied the Chrysos Heirs from all over the world once again, leading us to re-embark on this prophesized journey.
> **Castorice:** And they are the only two demigods in the holy city.

> *(Trailblazer)* Shouldn't it be the only four?
> **Phainon:** While Tribbie, Trianne, and Trinnon are three individuals, I suppose they are considered one single divine essence.
> *(Trailblazer)* So, what exactly is a demigod?
> **Phainon:** Worry not, I haven't forgotten about that.
> **Phainon:** In the past, the Titans were the pillars of the world. Yet, the prophecy's revelation is that these twelve pillars must be shattered and the world's order reconstructed.
> **Phainon:** But here's the problem: **After the fall of the old gods, someone must hold up the broken pillars and fill the vacant seats until the coming of the Miracle of Genesis.**
> **Phainon:** Otherwise, the entire world will collapse before the prophecy comes true.
> **Phainon:** Aglaea and Tribbie have already laid their own gods to rest, **making them now both human and divine, as well as the bearers of Amphoreus's fate.**

> *(Trailblazer)* Sounds like quite a responsibility
> *(Trailblazer)* Will you also become a demigod?
> *(Trailblazer)* You mentioned persuing the Strife Titan...
> **Dan Heng:** So, that's what you meant by "your Coreflame."
> **Phainon:** Bringing down the Titans, retrieving the Coreflames, and filling the vacant seats... This is the mission of the Chrysos Heirs, and I'm following in the footsteps of those two.
> **Castorice:** Mission, huh...
> **Phainon:** When it comes to the twelve Titans... no words can describe them more vividly than the whispers within the constellations.
> **Phainon:** Why not step forward and listen to them for yourselves?

### Part 21 — `databank/missions/chapter-01-heroic-saga.md`
**Context:** Chapter 1 — Heroic Saga of Flame-Chase (v3.0) > Mission 3 — Night Stars, Accompany My Slumber > Speak to Phainon About the Voices of Titans You Heard
**Kind:** collective-pointer
**Id:** `chapter-01-heroic-saga__speak-to-phainon-about-the-voices-of-titans-you-heard`
**Collective:** `databank/memories/collective/chapter-01-heroic-saga.md`
**Witness:** spoke

*(Phainon wanted you and Dan Heng to join the Chrysos Heirs and help defeat Nikador. If you decided to stay, Phainon and Castorice would try to patch things up with Aglaea. Dan Heng let you decide whether to stay or go.)*

**Phainon:** So, did you hear it?

> *(Trailblazer)* I did...
> *(Trailblazer)* I couldn't understand it...
> **Phainon:** The first time I heard the voices of the Titans, I was just a boy playing with swords, oblivious to the meaning of life.
> **Phainon:** To this day, I still don't know why the prophecy chose me, but I'm grateful for it... Without this journey, I might've already become a wandering soul on the battlefield.
> **Phainon:** Alright, that's enough of my story. I want to extend a heartfelt invitation to you: join the Chrysos Heirs, and aid us in hunting down Nikador and retrieving the Coreflame from the Titan of Strife.
> **Phainon:** If you still harbor doubts, I'll do my best to provide you with answers.

> *(Trailblazer)* How are the Chrysos Heirs chosen?
> **Castorice:** ...Unfortunately, the workings of the prophecy still remain a mystery to us all.
> **Castorice:** The only reliable speculation is that... **it favors those caught in dire circumstances and bestows miracles upon them.**
> *(Trailblazer)* Where are the other Chrysos Heirs in the world?
> **Phainon:** Legend has it that the first Chrysos Heir was born millennia ago. Since then, the chosen ones began to walk this earth.
> **Phainon:** Then came the Chrysos War... A conflict backed by the gods, yet one that brought much bloodshed and claimed many heroes.
> **Phainon:** By the time of my birth, the tales of the Chrysos Heirs had become more like legends than historical accounts. If there are others called forth by the prophecy... I trust that fate will guide them to us.
> *(Trailblazer)* How do you plan to fight Nikador?
> **Phainon:** Armed with courage and unwavering resolve, our quest will begin as soon as Trianne locates Castrum Kremnos.
> **Phainon:** Without a doubt, it will be a momentous battle to the death... Prepare yourselves.

> *(Trailblazer)* I've got it all figured out.
> **Phainon:** The path ahead is bound to be perilous, and I won't impose my will upon you. Yet, if you choose to stay, I, Phainon from Aedes Elysiae, will spare no effort to bridge the gap between you and the Chrysos Heirs.
> **Castorice:** Thawing the ice in Lady Aglaea's heart won't be easy...
> **Castorice:** But I will do my best as well.
> **Dan Heng:** ...(Trailblazer), I'll leave the final decision to you.

> *(Trailblazer)* You don't have any comments?
> **Dan Heng:** I trust Phainon, but I trust your judgment more.
> *(Trailblazer)* You're letting me make the decision? Seriously?
> **Dan Heng:** I trust your judgment more on matters like this.

> *(Trailblazer)* Stay on Amphoreus.
> **Phainon:** That's wonderful to hear. You truly are the hope of Amphoreus.
> **Phainon:** I've always believed that the journey of the Chrysos Heirs is not meant to be the saga of a select few, and that we must unite all potential allies, especially heroic figures like you.
> **Phainon:** Once we triumph in the battle against Nikador... that should dispel Aglaea's doubts.
> **Castorice:** Lord Phainon... Our guests seem quite weary. Should we have them return to the bath chamber for some rest?
> **Phainon:** Ah, my apologies. I got a bit carried away. It's been a long first day for you here on Amphoreus.
> **Phainon:** To leave the Vortex, simply repeat the same ritual in front of the spirit basin.
> **Phainon:** Be sure to rest well in the bath chamber. I trust that Trianne will have returned with news about Castrum Kremnos by the time the two of you are fully recharged.

> *(Trailblazer)* Leave Amphoreus.
> **Dan Heng:** So... you'll end things here? Are you sure about that?
> *(Trailblazer)* Just kidding. We have to press on in our trailblazing expedition.
> *(Trailblazer)* I'm tired. I just want to go.
> **Phainon:** ...I understand. Now that I think back on it, you both have selflessly helped Okhema, while we have met your good will with doubt and intimidation. It's indeed not fair.
> **Phainon:** You showed empathy toward my struggle earlier, and now it's my turn to return that kindness.
> **Castorice:** The journey of the Chrysos Heirs... truly is a lonely one.
> **Phainon:** Let's go, Dan Heng, (Trailblazer). I'll escort you back to where you landed.
> **Phainon:** I know that a reunion in the future is wishful thinking, but at the very least, we can bid each other a proper farewell.
> *(narrative)* Escorted by Phainon and the holy city guards, you and Dan Heng return to the crash site in the Abyss. Together, you salvage the Express' coach car.
> *(narrative)* With the assistance of an Okhema citizen resembling an Intellitron, you depart Amphoreus without suffering the so-called "curse from beyond the sky."
> *(narrative)* After returning to the Astral Express, you discover Black Swan has already left without telling anyone. March 7th's condition has gotten worse, but no one seems to know what the cause is.
> *(narrative)* Despite a fuel shortage, you complete a final warp jump, putting your hopes in the next location and how it might resolve all your worries.
> *(narrative)* As for the fate of Amphoreus, the Eternal Land, and the dramatic changes it will bring to the Cosmos... It has nothing to do with you, at least in your lifetime.
> *(narrative)* *Credits roll — "Amphoreus, The Eternal Land — The End — Honkai: Star Rail — Lead Actors: (Trailblazer) — Starring: Dan Heng as Dan Heng; Phainon as ??; Castorice as Castorice?; Tribios as Tribbie, Trinnon, Trianne ?? ?? ??...; Aglaea as Aglaea; Mydeimos as Mydei; ?? as ??; Nikador as Nikador — May This Journey Lead Us Starward."*
> *(narrative)* (Return to the starting screen; return to before speaking with Phainon, with the option removed)

---

**Dan Heng:** Well, that was a lot of drama for one day.

### Part 24 — `databank/missions/chapter-01-heroic-saga.md`
**Context:** Chapter 1 — Heroic Saga of Flame-Chase (v3.0) > Mission 3 — Night Stars, Accompany My Slumber > Go to the Chrysos Heirs Baths and Meet with Everyone
**Kind:** collective-pointer
**Id:** `chapter-01-heroic-saga__go-to-the-chrysos-heirs-baths-and-meet-with-everyone`
**Collective:** `databank/memories/collective/chapter-01-heroic-saga.md`
**Witness:** spoke

**Dan Heng:** I wasn't expecting the expedition to set off after only a night of rest.
**Dan Heng:** Let's set the questions aside for now. The Chrysos Heirs are getting ready to mobilize.

*(You met the Chrysos Heirs at the baths. As they prepared to fight Nikador, they chose you, Phainon, and Mydei for the mission, with Trianne opening the way to Castrum Kremnos.)*

**Phainon:** Great to see you here!
**Mydei:** Hmph... Entrusting our fates to two strangers, is this madness or folly?
**Phainon:** Maybe both. You should really make more friends, Mydei.
**Aglaea:** Heroes, your attention please.
**Aglaea:** Trianne has returned with news: For the first time in many years, we have dispersed the fog around Castrum Kremnos and verified its location.
**Aglaea:** The awaited moment has finally arrived for the Chrysos Heirs. Our path forward is now clear: Vanquish Nikador, the violent Lance of Fury, and bring the Coreflame of Strife back to Okhema to fuel the prophesized miracle.
**Aglaea:** This is an arduous mission, and I must heed the voices of all to determine the best candidates for this campaign.
**Aglaea:** Should you harbor any thoughts, this is the moment to bring them forth.
**Phainon:** I, Phainon of Aedes Elysiae, wish to join the crusade against Nikador.
**Phainon:** Furthermore, I recommend the two warriors from foreign lands. They have fought alongside me against Nikador's double, and I have full faith in their bravery.

> *(Trailblazer)* That sounds really dangerous. Is it too late to back out?
> **Dan Heng:** Lounging in a bath chamber isn't exactly trailblazing.
> *(Trailblazer)* Guess we have no choice but to give it our all.
> **Dan Heng:** Are you still not used to it? This is the destiny of a Trailblazer.

**Mydei:** I, Mydei from Castrum Kremnos, volunteer to join.
**Mydei:** Nikador has become corrupted over the years, his pride waning, but his might endures. Victory only lies in assembling a full-fledged army.
**Castorice:** ...I'm willing to join as well, to help guide departing souls and ease everyone's burdens...
**Aglaea:** I concur with your opinion. Though his divinity has faded, Nikador remains a formidable adversary.
**Aglaea:** Yet, I cannot jeopardize all of your lives in one single battle.
**Aglaea:** As we speak, the Hand of Shadow remains missing, the Eye of Twilight is still coveting our land, and Cerces and Oronyx present significant threats, despite their lack of overt hostility toward humanity.
**Aglaea:** Even with two demigods protecting the holy city, the risk is too great. Hence...
**Aglaea:** Castorice, you must remain here. Your place is not on the front lines, and the battlefield is best left to the warriors.
**Castorice:** ...I understand.
**Aglaea:** As for the two foreign warriors... Select one to accompany you, Phainon. Placing both esteemed guests on the front line would not be proper.
**Phainon:** (Trailblazer) excels in wielding a range of weapons, and his flaming lance has quelled the enemy's advances multiple times. Mydei and I are both better at offense, so from a tactical standpoint...
**Phainon:** (Trailblazer), would you be willing to fight alongside us?

> *(Trailblazer)* Sure, let's do this!
> *(Trailblazer)* I might not be tanky enough, though.
> **Dan Heng:** With those two, the battle should end quickly.
> *(Trailblazer)* We could try letting Dan Heng tank...
> **Dan Heng:** What are you talking about? Now's not the time for jokes.
> **Phainon:** If (Trailblazer) has no objections... Aglaea, the three of us will take on the mission to hunt down the Titan.

**Aglaea:** Mydeimos, Castrum Kremnos was your home. Your people were once Nikador's followers, yet you've chosen to turn to Okhema and dedicate yourself to the prophecy.
**Aglaea:** Do you swear upon Kephale's body to make this mission of the Chrysos Heirs your foremost duty?
**Mydei:** When Nikador succumbed to madness, they stopped being a god to my people.
**Mydei:** I will use my wrath as a human to strip that god of their authority. As for the Coreflame and that soon-to-be-vacant divine throne...
**Mydei:** If that is the object you desire, you can have it. I have no interest in becoming a replacement god.
**Phainon:** Hah! How generous, but I'm not the type to accept charity, so I'll definitely be the one who deals the final blow through Nikador's heart.
**Aglaea:** The party that will embark on the expedition to Castrum Kremnos is decided: Phainon from Aedes Elysiae, Mydei from Castrum Kremnos, and... (Trailblazer) from the outlands.

### Part 25 — `databank/missions/chapter-01-heroic-saga.md`
**Context:** Chapter 1 — Heroic Saga of Flame-Chase (v3.0) > Mission 3 — Night Stars, Accompany My Slumber > Speak with Trianne and Get Ready to Go to Castrum Kremnos > Talk to Dan Heng, optional
**Kind:** collective-pointer

**Trianne:** Blow, wind, blow~! All the way to Castrum Kremnos!
**Trianne:** Woohoo! Snowy, De, let's fly together! All the way across!
**Phainon:** We don't have wings on our backs, Trianne. Can you open a gate for us?
**Trianne:** Oh... A gate. How boring. Alright, let's open a gate! Century Gate! Woo!!

> *(Trailblazer)* Why does she act like a child?
> *(Trailblazer)* Oh, it's not an actual gate?
> **Phainon:** Hahaha, were you expecting actual city gates?
> **Phainon:** It's true Trianne is very playful, but she's the best gatekeeper in Amphoreus. Her Century Gate never fails.

> *(Trailblazer)* There's a chance of failure?
> *(Trailblazer)* ...Is there another way to travel?
> **Mydei:** Instead of worrying about gates, why don't you take the time to think about how the three of us are supposed to fend off an entire army.
> **Phainon:** That's not like you at all, Mydei. Don't tell me you're scared?
> **Trianne:** Alright! Century Gate... is opening... soon! Right now!

**Trianne:** Snowy, De, and newbie Little Gray, are you all ready?

> *(Trailblazer)* Let's go slay a god!
> **Trianne:** Slay, slay!
> **Dan Heng:** (Trailblazer), take this with you.
> *(narrative)* Dan Heng gives March 7th's camera to you...
> **Dan Heng:** Record the sights you see on your adventure for March.
> *(Trailblazer)* Wait for my triumphant return!
> *(Trailblazer)* I'll capture all the decisive moments!
> **Dan Heng:** Keep yourself safe, and work within your capabilities.
> **Trianne:** Ahem! Stand still and listen up!
> **Trianne:** We — Tribbie, Trianne, and Trinnon from Janusopolis, messengers of the Chrysos Heirs and the demigods who bear Janus's divine authority — give unto you our blessings!
> **Trianne:** May fate show you benevolence and seal away all paths that lead to dead ends...
> **Trianne:** And throw open all gates that lead to victory!

> *(Trailblazer)* I still need a moment to mentally prepare...
> **Trianne:** Get ready! Get ready! I don't wanna wait! Slowpoke!
> **Phainon:** I'm sorry, Trianne. This is a matter of grave importance, so please bear with us.

### Part 26 — `databank/missions/chapter-01-heroic-saga.md`
**Context:** Chapter 1 — Heroic Saga of Flame-Chase (v3.0) > Mission 4 — Wasteland, Hark Back Glory of Old > Continue Onward and Explore Castrum Kremnos
**Kind:** collective-pointer
**Id:** `chapter-01-heroic-saga__continue-onward-and-explore-castrum-kremnos`
**Collective:** `databank/memories/collective/chapter-01-heroic-saga.md`
**Witness:** spoke

*(Trailblazer, Phainon, and Mydei arrive at Castrum Kremnos. What was once a fierce city filled with the clamor of armaments has become a haunting ruin. Deep within this mobile fortress lies the maddened and fallen Nikador.)*

*(With Phainon and Mydei, you reached Castrum Kremnos and found the moving fortress in ruins, its outer corridors unguarded. You headed inside.)*

**Phainon:** ...
**Phainon:** It's quieter than I expected...
**Mydei:** It's corrupted... but still familiar.
**Mydei:** This is Castrum Kremnos.
**Phainon:** Times really have changed. Even Nikador's own lands are on the verge of collapse. I haven't seen any sign of Titankin.
**Mydei:** If even half of Castrum Kremnos' old might remains, it would mean that Aglaea sent us to our deaths.
**Mydei:** This is the outer city corridor. It used to be heavily guarded by soldiers. Even with the two of us working together, we wouldn't have made it past thirty steps.

> *(Trailblazer)* What if you added me to the count?
> *(Trailblazer)* Is seeing all this making you homesick?
> **Mydei:** Think whatever you want.

**Phainon:** Yeah, Kremnos used to be the place every warrior longed for.
**Phainon:** Kind of a shame you didn't live in that era full of strong warriors, eh Mydei? If all the heroes of the past hadn't fallen one after another, I wouldn't be the only worthy opponent you have left.
**Mydei:** Are you scared? It's still not too late to run.
**Phainon:** No, I'm just thinking about the warriors who also braved this very corridor in the past. They all ventured towards the city of the Lance of Fury, all just to set their names in gold.
**Phainon:** The fortress may be in ruins now, but what we're about to do is no different from what they were striving for back then.
**Phainon:** Let's go. We'll take back the Coreflame of Strife or die in Kremnos.
**Phainon:** Those are the only two paths to glory we can choose from.

---

**Phainon:** How does it feel to be home again after so long?
**Mydei:** Not here. I lived in the inner city.
**Phainon:** Oh... As befitting of the crown prince of Kremnos.

---

### Part 27 — `databank/missions/chapter-01-heroic-saga.md`
**Context:** Chapter 1 — Heroic Saga of Flame-Chase (v3.0) > Mission 4 — Wasteland, Hark Back Glory of Old > Continue Onward and Explore Castrum Kremnos > Talk to Trianne, optional
**Kind:** collective-pointer
**Id:** `chapter-01-heroic-saga__continue-onward-and-explore-castrum-kremnos-2`
**Collective:** `databank/memories/collective/chapter-01-heroic-saga.md`
**Witness:** spoke

*(Talk to Trianne, optional)*
**Phainon:** Trianne, the road ahead is dangerous. You should stay here and find a safe place to hide.
**Phainon:** We'll return once we complete our mission. Then we'll have to trouble you to use the Century Gate again, alright?
**Trianne:** Got it. Hide so the baddies can't find me!

> *(Trailblazer)* Is she really a demigod...
> *(Trailblazer)* She still looks and acts just like a child.
> **Phainon:** Trianne has sacrificed a lot for Amphoreus.

*(Investigate the mural, optional)*
**Phainon:** By the way, aren't you going to tell (Trailblazer) any stories about Castrum Kremnos? Aren't you worried I'll tell him all the historical myths you despise?
**Mydei:** I'd like to see you try.
**Phainon:** *sigh* It's exhausting talking to you sometimes...

> *(Trailblazer)* Tell me about this one.
> *(Trailblazer)* Is this Tetris?
> **Phainon:** Castrum Kremnos has been Nikador's territory since ancient times. It's a massive moving fortress that surrounds the Titan, built by their followers.
> **Phainon:** In the age before the Lance of Fury fell from grace, Castrum Kremnos would follow the Lance into battle after battle. It's a city that enforces strict discipline and reveres military might.
> **Phainon:** Compared to that, the customs of my home feel very unsophisticated.

---

*(Mydei pointed out the main gate and told you the acropolis was just beyond it.)*

**Mydei:** There. Once we pass through that gate, we'll be in the acropolis.
**Phainon:** We'll finally see some soldiers roaming about.
**Mydei:** They're minions who've succumbed to depravity. Smashing them to pieces would be the greatest mercy.

---

*(The bridge to the outer city, which criminals had built, was broken and blocked your way. You offered to use Oronyx's Prayer to fix it.)*

**Phainon:** It seems enemies aren't the only things stopping us. This bridge has fallen into disrepair. I suppose even Kremnoan craftsmanship can be unreliable sometimes.
**Mydei:** Sorry to disappoint, but the outer city was built by convicts.
**Phainon:** No wonder it's so fragile. Maybe these people were taking their grievances out on you.
**Mydei:** Doesn't matter. There's no shame in resistance here, whether it's from an adversary or a convict on death row.
**Phainon:** Except now, it's stopping the people trying to kill Nikador. It's moments like this that make me miss Tribbie's power.

> *(Trailblazer)* You never learned the prayer?
> **Phainon:** Learning and mastering are two different concepts. Without a priest's resonance, an ordinary person would fall into a coma after chanting the first few words of the prayer.
> *(Trailblazer)* Lucky for you, I'm not an ordinary person.
> *(Trailblazer)* Let me do it!
> **Phainon:** Oh? Tribbie taught you how?
> *(Trailblazer)* Lucky for you, I'm an expert.
> **Phainon:** Oh? Tribbie taught you how?
> **Phainon:** I have to say that the ability to perform miracles is innate. Without a priest's resonance, an ordinary person would fall into a coma after chanting the first few words of the prayer.
> **Phainon:** But since you've volunteered, please be my guest.

*(After repairing the bridge)*
**Phainon:** Amazing! You do have the touch!

*(After crossing the bridge)*
**Phainon:** And it's broken again... I guess even a Titan's power isn't enough to stabilize this bridge.
**Mydei:** Just like the soul of a criminal: fragile.

*(After interacting with the switch near the gate)*
**Phainon:** Look, the gate opened—
**Mydei:** Genius. We never would have known if you hadn't said anything.
**Phainon:** Let's go. We can take a detour from the broken bridge.

*(After going through the gate)*
**Phainon:** Look, in the sky! That's...
**Mydei:** The Blade of Fury — Even if the world lay shattered, it would still hang high above everyone's heads.

---

**Phainon:** It's magnificent...
**Mydei:** You haven't seen it at its most magnificent.
**Mydei:** That's the giant sword Nikador used to destroy Aquila's heavenly kingdom long ago, followed by one city-state after another.
**Mydei:** It's not just a weapon, it's the faith of the Kremnoan people. Souls who died heroically on the battlefield will further temper the blade in Nikador's hand, becoming a part of the god-king's power.
**Mydei:** Such is the Titan's conquest — Nikador, the Titankin, and the people who have faith in Strife sweep away all their enemies. Even in death, they embrace eternal glory.
**Phainon:** I think you're exaggerating a little. I can think of at least three losses suffered by Castrum Kremnos in the Chrysos War, just off the top of my head.
**Mydei:** There's no shame in losing to those who conspire and plot in secret. Those weak and despicable individuals are the ones who should be ashamed.
**Phainon:** True. Valorous Death Before Glorious Return. The Kremnoan people have always been straightforward. They spend their whole lives running to the battlefield. No wonder Zagreus loves teasing you.
**Phainon:** ...But that's all in the past.
**Phainon:** Guess what I'm thinking, Mydei?
**Mydei:** Probably nothing good. Keep it simple.
**Phainon:** This isn't just an expedition. It's also a battle to determine a victor. Since we're already here, why don't we have a match and fight to our heart's content?
**Phainon:** Just you and me. Let's recreate the original Kremnos Festival.
**Phainon:** Show me what the grandest fighting competition in Castrum Kremnos was like. Let's gild this expedition with another layer of glory and **compete to see who can kill the most crazed Titankin**. How about it?
**Mydei:** What's the prize?
**Phainon:** The privilege of delivering the final blow to Nikador.
**Mydei:** Interesting. I accept your challenge.

> *(Trailblazer)* What about me?
> *(Trailblazer)* Am I the referee?
> **Phainon:** I know Mydei. He prefers to fight alone. There are two paths ahead. (Trailblazer) and I will take the same one.
> **Phainon:** We won't compete on who's fastest but on the number of trophies, so we'll each be responsible for our own tally. Don't worry. I won't add his kills to my own count.
> **Mydei:** Doesn't matter to me even if you do.
> **Mydei:** You don't know the lay of the land, so I'll give you a ten-step head start.
> **Phainon:** Haha! He already thinks he's won, but I won't decline the offer either. Let's get going, (Trailblazer)!

---

**Phainon:** Let's go! We're going to win this competition!

*(Begin battle)*

*(narrative)* You and Phainon defeated 4 Titankins together, and you collected Kremnos crests from their bodies...

**Phainon:** That's two down. Not a bad start.

> *(Trailblazer)* But I counted four...

> *(Trailblazer)* Isn't it more dangerous to split up?
> **Phainon:** Don't worry. Mydei is strong, and so am I.

> **Phainon:** Nothing is more important to Mydei than his people and glory. The city may be in ruins, but... Kremnos is still the only place where he can be free.
> **Phainon:** Now that we've sent him away, he can go have a good cry wherever he wants.

> *(Trailblazer)* So, you two are actually really close.

> **Phainon:** We've been both friends and foes since the day we met. Working with him always brings out my competitive side.

**Mydei:** Why are you just standing there? Giving up already?
**Phainon:** What do you know? We're talking strategy here!
**Phainon:** Let's go, (Trailblazer). The competition might not be the real goal, but I still really hate losing.

*(After heading through the second door)*
**Phainon:** Whoa... That's impressive.
**Phainon:** Following an iron chain, onto the path leading to the giant sword on the other side? This is more like it!

*(Investigate the spot, optional)*
*(narrative)* The Kremnoan follows you with his eagle-sharp gaze. You're sure he can keep his eyes open day and night without blinking.

**Mydei:** And just so you know: I'm at six.
**Phainon:** Wow, seems like luck is on your side. Our path was a little lax on security. Not to worry though, I'll catch up once we cross this iron chain bridge.
**Mydei:** Assuming you can get across. Do you see the lightning bolts striking the chain?
**Phainon:** It doesn't look like ordinary lightning, which means mindlessly running across would spell certain death.
**Phainon:** There happens to be a Prophecy Tablet here though. Let's go take a look. Maybe it'll help us.

> **Phainon:** In principle, there should be an instrument used by Time Priests somewhere in this fortress... Aha, there it is.

*(You interacted with the mysterious device, hoping it would work.)*

*(Approach Oronyx's Shrine)*
**Phainon:** What did I tell you? I get especially lucky when I'm with you. This instrument stabilizes the connection between priests and the Three Titans of Fate. Give it a try.

> *(Trailblazer)* Are there any side effects?
> **Phainon:** Relax. If something should happen to you, "I'll be sure to pass on your last words to Dan Heng, my friend!"
> *(Trailblazer)* If something should happen to me...
> **Phainon:** "I'll be sure to pass on your last words to Dan Heng, my friend!"
> **Phainon:** Don't worry. You'll be just fine.

*(After activating the shrine)*
**Phainon:** Not bad. Something feels different about you already.
**Phainon:** Why don't you give it another go?

*(After successfully rewinding the ball)*
**Phainon:** That's it! Nice work.

*(Continue down the hall into the main area)*
*(narrative)* ...!
**Phainon:** From the sound of things, we're about to lose if we don't step it up.

*(Approach a landing)*
**Phainon:** Careful. We're getting very close to the Blade of Fury...

---

*(You and Phainon ran into a bunch of enemies and saw a chance to beat Mydei's score. This path would take you to the Kremnos Arena.)*

**Phainon:** This is the center all right. That's a sizable formation.

> *(Trailblazer)* There's a lot of them...
> **Phainon:** In other words, this is our chance to overtake Mydei.
> *(Trailblazer)* Now there's a chance to take the lead!
> **Phainon:** My thoughts exactly. You have the heart of a fighter, my friend.
> **Phainon:** If the records are correct, directly beneath the Blade of Fury lies the world-renowned Kremnos Arena. The path before us leads either to glory or to death...
> **Phainon:** Of course, glory is ours, and death is theirs!

*(Begin battle)*
**Phainon:** "Approaching now are the duo of gladiators whose names strike terror in all who hear them — the one and only Dark Swordmaster, Phainon!"
**Trailblazer:** "And the Galactic Baseballer!"
**Phainon:** "What a savage hit! The Baseballer shows no mercy to their enemies. Can you hear that? The crowd's roaring in approval!"
**Trailblazer:** "Hmm... This requires a bit of imagination."
**Phainon:** "What marvelous teamwork! These two gladiators..."
**Mydei:** These delusions of yours are seriously getting on my nerves!
**Phainon:** "...must also contend with the noise from outside the arena. How unfortunate!"

*(After the battle)*
**Phainon:** "With their exceptional strength and perfect teamwork, these two gladiators have bested every opponent..."
**Mydei:** "...but the glory they've been striving for has not come as anticipated."
**Phainon:** How disappointing — no sign of the Titan at all. Is Nikador hiding somewhere?
**Mydei:** They're most likely in the Soul-Forging Zone.
**Mydei:** The path is right behind you.
**Phainon:** Thanks. What about you?
**Mydei:** I'll take the other path. It's crawling with parasites.
**Phainon:** You've definitely had the upper hand in this competition. (Trailblazer), shall we go?
**Phainon:** Well, it's not every day you see a view like this. Go ahead and capture it with the photostone.

> *(Trailblazer)* She is likely not interested in such scenery.
> *(Trailblazer)* I'll take a photo of it then.

---

*(You and Phainon ran into Mydei again, who said he'd beaten thirty enemies. When Phainon showed him the tablet with ancient Kremnoan writing, Mydei just teased him for being an "amateur historian.")*

*(Continue through the second door, onto the bridge)*
**Phainon:** We meet again! What's your count so far?
**Mydei:** Thirty.
**Phainon:** That's not too far off. We still stand a chance.
**Phainon:** I just remembered — we came across a slate with inscriptions in an ancient language of Castrum Kremnos. Could you help translate it?
**Mydei:** Is it beyond your knowledge, amateur historian?
**Phainon:** Sadly, yes. You are my only hope now.
**Mydei:** You just keep having fun. I've got better things to do.

*(Begin descending the staircase towards mural)*

**Phainon:** Looks like they're creating Titankin. Maybe this is what "Soul-Forging" means?
**Phainon:** Crafting a body from rough stones and then infusing it with a god's golden blood to bring it to life...
**Phainon:** No wonder Castrum Kremnos keeps winning battle after battle. With this method, they can create as many soldiers as they want...

---

*(You and Phainon found Mydei in the Stockpiling Zone, not far from Nikador. You knew this was your last chance to collect any treasures.)*

**Phainon:** How did you beat us here again? But there are still plenty of enemies around. What's wrong? Already tired?
**Mydei:** I gave you two this final chance on purpose. If you're not interested, fine.
**Phainon:** Well, how generous of you. This is the Stockpiling Zone — we must be getting close to Nikador.
**Mydei:** That's right. But by the time you arrive, he might already have fallen by my hand.
**Phainon:** Then we must act quickly. We can't let you die right before our eyes, can we?

**Phainon:** We'll be meeting up with Mydei soon. **Want to clean up this area and increase our trophy count?**
**Phainon:** Victory or defeat, it all comes down to this, (Trailblazer).

*(You and Phainon came to a cliff edge. Phainon found some control devices for the Hand of Zagreus nearby that might help you bridge the gap.)*

**Phainon:** The path is cut off. But how did he get through? Did... he set up this roadblock on purpose?

> *(Trailblazer)* Kremnoan people sure are cunning!
> **Phainon:** He might look honest, but he's a lot shrewder than he seems.
> *(Trailblazer)* It's all in your head.
> **Phainon:** He might look honest, but he's a lot shrewder than he seems.
> *(Trailblazer)* Let me jump across—
> **Phainon:** Hold your horses! We're not exactly immortal here.
> **Phainon:** Enough joking. Let's look around...
> **Phainon:** This might come in handy — the stone table for the Hand of Zagreus. Let's use it to fill the crack in the path.

> *(Trailblazer)* The Hand of Zagreus?
> **Phainon:** Simply put, Zagreus is the Titan of trickery — thievery, deception, and betrayal, these unsavory acts are all tied to them.
> **Phainon:** That Titan has fallen, but they left "Bewitching Hands" scattered all over the world. Now, people use these giant hands to move things around — and, well, to steal things, too.
> *(Trailblazer)* Let's get a move on. I'll stop with the questions.
> **Phainon:** I don't normally approve of this, but given the circumstances, let's use it this one time.

**Phainon:** We need to find a solution to mend the broken path.
*(Lay bridge with Hand of Zagreus)*
**Phainon:** Flawless work. Great job!

*(After exiting the room)*
**Phainon:** So many stones here. I wonder how many war machines can be built with all this?
**Phainon:** But after today, they'll be slumbering here forever.

**Mydei:** Nikador's soul should be claimed by true warriors.

*(You, Phainon, and Mydei found a Chryseus Leo that told you Nikador was planning something sinister. It begged Mydei to kill Nikador so that the Strife Titan could die as a warrior should.)*

**Phainon:** Wow, look at this big guy. Could this be Verax Leo?
**Mydei:** Show some respect. This is Chryseus Leo, not some rumor-spouting gossip-monger.
**Mydei:** Chryseus Leo was the top advisor in Castrum Kremnos, whose wise counsel helped my tribe win many battles and thwart numerous schemes.
**Mydei:** Sadly, he has lost his voice now. If only the Chrysos Heirs could benefit from his wisdom—
**Chryseus Leo:** M—Mydeimos...
**Chryseus Leo:** Crown... prince?

**Chryseus Leo:** Crown prince... Castrum Kremnos has lost everything — its people, traditions... and most importantly, its glory.

**Chryseus Leo:** Our god has completely lost their sanity. No longer a revered symbol of war, they have fallen and are now plotting an evil scheme...
**Chryseus Leo:** I urge you, Mydeimos, to put an end to their endless suffering. Allow them to die as a warrior! They must... go with honor...
**Chryseus Leo:** ...
**Mydei:** ...Teacher?
**Mydei:** Teacher, answer me! As your crown prince, I command you—
**Phainon:** ...Mydei.
**Phainon:** Let him rest. He must be exhausted.
**Mydei:** ...
**Mydei:** ...Perhaps those were just the delirious words of a dying lion. I shouldn't overthink it. Let's keep going.
**Phainon:** Hold on. This is the slate that I mentioned earlier. We couldn't decipher it.
**Phainon:** Maybe it's connected to the scheme the old lion mentioned.
**Mydei:** Hand it to me.

*(Quick Read — Castrum Kremnos' Archaic Language Slate)*
> *...The black tide may be an indescribable enemy, but if exploited carefully, it can also become a sharp weapon pointed at our archenemy.*
>
> *Infiltrators can be instructed to transport a load of tainted Titankin covered in special earth, so that they may be smuggled among dromas caravan goods into Okhema. Upon entry, under the cover of the Curtain-Fall Hour, the personnel will stash the kin in various sections of the city. After a few days, the earth will weather and peel, suffocating the streets of Okhema with Titankin's tainted air.*
>
> *When this moment arrives, the Okhemans will be frantically searching for the source of the pollution. We just have to wait for the chaos to unfurl to take the final step to victory: Gather the heroic souls within the city, let the divine blade of wrath point at Okhema, and run Strife's blade through the entire city — after which, our forces will overwhelm them like an ocean wave crashing into them, and the long-awaited victory will finally fall into our grasp.*

**Mydei:** No... Nikador would never...
**Phainon:** That explains the surprise attack on Okhema. I didn't think that the mad Titan could have orchestrated it...
**Mydei:** Okhema is on the brink of destruction. Someone needs to go back and rescue them.
**Phainon:** ...
**Phainon:** N—No, we need to press on and finish our mission.
**Mydei:** Are you blinded by glory? As I said before, victory only lies in assembling a full-fledged army.
**Phainon:** We're just one step away from Nikador. Defeat the source of the evil, and the threat will vanish on its own.
**Phainon:** Just like that decisive strike, there is no turning back for us. Nikador could drive Okhema to the brink of ruin, but we can do the same to them.
**Phainon:** Besides, I have faith in Aglaea and Tribbie. With two demigods and our allies watching over Okhema, they should be more than capable of protecting the holy city.

> *(Trailblazer)* Dan Heng is there too.
> *(Trailblazer)* We can't let our efforts go to waste.
> **Mydei:** ...
> **Mydei:** ...Alright, I'll trust your judgment this time. Let's hope we don't end up in an abyss of regrets.
> **Mydei:** Nikador... Has your divine body truly been hollowed out by the hands of madness? Have you completely forsaken your dignity as a warrior?

*(Obtain Castrum Kremnos' Archaic Language Slate)*

---

*(Through Chryseus Leo, Trailblazer and the party learned that the maddened Nikador is planning a devastating attack on Okhema. With events taking this dire turn, their only option is to press forward and eliminate the source of this malevolence.)*

*(Exit the building)*
**Mydei:** The sweltering heat — the Titan's wrath has set the air ablaze.
**Phainon:** Yeah, we're getting pretty close—

---

*(You went into the Sculpting Zone with Phainon and Mydei. Strife's corruption scrambled Phainon's thoughts, and he had trouble focusing while suffering the intense heat.)*

**Phainon:** It's getting even hotter. Is this place still in use?
**Mydei:** After we pass through here, we'll reach the Soul-Forging Zone.
**Phainon:** The heat is unbearable, and all these thoughts are echoing in my head...
**Phainon:** On second thought, this could very well be where we meet our end, right?
**Mydei:** Be careful. Your thoughts are being corrupted by Strife.
**Phainon:** You're right. It was my decision to keep pushing forward. And with (Trailblazer) here, we're sure to complete our mission and return safely.
**Phainon:** Just like the path ahead, even though it's completely dilapidated, it will still lead us to our final destination.

*(Approach the Hand of Zagreus control platform)*
**Mydei:** Let me smash it—
**Phainon:** Hold your horses! Don't waste your energy here — use your head.
**Phainon:** Looks like we'll need to use the hand again.

*(Head down the Hand of Zagreus)*

**Mydei:** Put your sword away... It seems to still be rational.
**Mydei:** We might be able to get some clues from it.

*(A Strife Titankin stood in your way but didn't attack. Mydei asked them about Nikador's plan, but they just mumbled the hymn to Strife.)*

**Sculpting Zone Strong Man:** Immortal soul of war, ever by my side.

> **Mydei:** What do you propose? I'm all ears.
> *(Trailblazer)* This might be above its pay grade.
> **Mydei:** There aren't any other options.

**Sculpting Zone Strong Man:** All hail Nikador, my master and guide.
**Sculpting Zone Strong Man:** Immortal soul of war, ever by my side.

**Mydei:** Let's keep going. There might be other Titankin nearby we can talk to.

**Mydei:** If you see it as a threat, go ahead. I have no reason to do it.

**Phainon:** Aside from one's mission and survival, no other reason can justify violence.

**Mydei:** ...

**Sculpting Zone Supervisor:** For the Lance of Fury...
**Sculpting Zone Supervisor:** Consecrate your souls of war!
