**Anaxa:** Now, listen up, and listen well! I, Anaxagoras of the Grove of Epiphany, hereby declare:

*(narrative)* *crowd chatter*
**Caenis:** What!? Are you saying that we must bow down to you and worship you even in our next lives!?
**Caenis:** Enough, you blasphemous imbecile! How dare you try to deceive us with all these lies and falsehoods! How can you prove—
**Anaxa:** I told you, don't interrupt me! But since you've asked, I shall be kind and indulge you: My statements are based on my experiments on the divine body of Kephale themself!
**Anaxa:** You heard me correctly, everyone! With the tacit approval of Caenis and the Council of Elders, I fused my soul with a god through alchemy and learned the truth behind Era Nova!
**Confused Citizen:** What is he talking about...?
**Confused Citizen:** Isn't that pure blasphemy?
**Anaxa:** I, the vile, wretched blasphemer, deserve nothing less than death for my sins...
**Anaxa:** And the Council of Elders, which granted me consent and aided me in committing this atrocity, should face punishment alongside me!
**Dramatic Citizen:** By the gods, even Caenis has betrayed Okhema...
*(narrative)* *crowd chatter*
**Caenis:** I—I had no knowledge of his transgressions...! He did that on his own! The Council of Elders bears no responsibility for his actions!
**Caenis:** Lord Lygus... As the esteemed Theoros, are you going to simply stand by and allow the Council of Elders to be dragged through the mud!?
**Lygus:** If the accusation is true, I will acknowledge it. If not, my conscience stands unburdened. Lady Caenis, the reputation of the Council of Elders was not built in a day, nor will it be shattered overnight.
**Lygus:** Repercussions await those who fool the people, and punishment must be meted out to those who abandon their vows. We should respect this beautiful city and her people.
**Caenis:** You...
**Lygus:** Lady Aglaea, the Goldweaver, I humbly ask that you take on the responsibility of overseeing this judgment, in the name of the fairness of Talanton and the justice of Kephale...
**Lygus:** ...Considering the circumstances, and in accordance with Okhema's Codex, as the only demigod among us, you shall rightfully wield this authority.
**Aglaea:** ...The honor is mine to bear.

---

*(narrative)* *crowd chatter*
**Aglaea:** Elders and citizens, silence, if you please!
**Aglaea:** Anaxagoras of the Grove of Epiphany has made his statement clear. As per Okhema's Codex, I request all present citizens to stand with me in exercising the right of judgment.
**Aglaea:** First, Elder Caenis of Okhema and Lygus the Theoros stand accused of neglecting their duties and abusing the law, leading to the egregious blasphemy against a god...
**Aglaea:** In the names of Talanton and Kephale, Okhema is to form a committee before the seventh Action Hour to conduct an investigation and disclose the findings to the public for subsequent judgment!
**Citizens' Assembly:** Agreed! Agreed!
**Caenis:** Curse you...!
**Aglaea:** Now, concerning Anaxagoras of the Grove of Epiphany, he has confessed to grave blasphemy against Kephale...
**Aglaea:** In the names of Talanton and Kephale, the accused must be allowed to hear the "people's voice" prior to the execution for a chance at a reduced sentence and possible pardon!
**Citizens' Assembly:** Let him die! Let him die!
**Anaxa:** ...
**Phainon:** Professor...
**Aglaea:** ...

### Part 95 — `databank/missions/chapter-03-through-petals.md`
**Context:** Chapter 3 — Through the Petals in the Land of Repose (v3.2) > Sub-Mission 11: Scholar, Let Us Meet Again Before the Gates of Truth > Submit Coreflame

**Aglaea:** But... I don't see Castorice among your number.
**Anaxa:** It looks like she not only helped me prove my theory, but helped you fulfill your mission as well.

> *(Trailblazer)* She finally understood her origins and completed her trial.
> *(Trailblazer)* I believe she'll always be at our side.
> **Phainon:** ...I agree.
> **Aglaea:** She might not have been able to change her fate, but she still wrote the final verse of her epic herself.
> **Aglaea:** Trinnon, please recite the prophecy for us. (Trailblazer), please surrender that scalding Coreflame for us as well.
> **Aglaea:** You are the only life she ever saved by her own hand in her one thousand years on this earth. I believe no one is more qualified for this noble task.
> *(Trailblazer)* Let the ceremony begin.

**Trinnon:** O! Gods who have returned, and gods who are still watching! Today, we bring you a soul laid bare.
**Trinnon:** The Hand of Shadow, Thanatos, remembered their name from the past! From daylight they came and to their fragrant dwelling they return, to walk now among your ranks.
**Trinnon:** And there was one who joined hands with the Titan. A traveler risen from the dead and reborn among mortal kind. She is the everlasting night and shall now proclaim in her good name:
**Trinnon:** "O majesty of the Twelve Titans, pillars of the world—"
**Trinnon:** "We seek your divinity, to mend the rifts of the world —"
**Trinnon:** "Fill our bodies with blood of gold, till we wither in willing service to the prophecy..."
**Trinnon:** Step forward, Trailblazer from beyond the sky. And together with **us**...
**Trinnon:** Surrender the Coreflame and carve their star into the sky.

> *(Trailblazer)* Surrender the Coreflame.
> *(Trailblazer)* Goodbye, Castorice.

*(Submit Coreflame of Death)*

---

*(The constellation representing Death is illuminated)*
**Phainon:** A constellation for Death...
**Anaxa:** For Life and Death, to be more precise.
**Phainon:** Look! It's...
**Divine Echo:** ...
**Dan Heng:** Is that... Castorice?
**Anaxa:** No, it's only a Divine Echo. I think she's here for the trial.
**Divine Echo:** *Titan's murmurs*
**(Trailblazer):** ...
**Phainon:** What did she say?
**Trinnon:** That's the language of the Titans...
**Trinnon:** Castorice... No, the Titan...
**Trinnon:** They said...
**Divine Echo:** "...I have illuminated the cold shadow of death."
**Divine Echo:** "Onwards. For your path to the future is bright and its flame eternal."
**Divine Echo:** "Do not forget..."

---

**Anaxa:** Heh, she made quite a scene. I suppose it's my turn now.
**Aglaea:** I won't stop you...
**Phainon:** Professor, you...
**Anaxa:** What's with that look? You were present.
**Anaxa:** So, let's get this execution over with. We wouldn't want to bring shame to Okhema's divine laws or keep me from creating a new world.
**Phainon:** You never change, do you, Professor?
**Phainon:** I don't understand. If your theory is correct, you'll lose all memories of your past life once you become a Titan, so what's the point of becoming a god?
**Anaxa:** Of course there's a point. Even if I forget who I am, I'll still have my brilliant mind.
**Anaxa:** It goes without saying I'll find a way to retrieve memories of my past life. Besides, I still have you, don't I?
**Phainon:** I still don't really understand what you said during the assembly. Even if it were true, things reforged from memory could never be the same as the original anyway.
**Anaxa:** Then allow me to tell you right now that you're wrong.
**Anaxa:** What exactly are we? My answer is that the Chrysos Heirs are the future Titans, and vice versa. Of course, this answer only applies to us.
**Anaxa:** So, what are "we" when it comes to regular mortals?
**Anaxa:** **The very essence of the world — our souls — are tiny invisible seeds that hold records of each person's memories of the world, and "we" are the buds that sprout from these seeds to grow into great trees.**
**Phainon:** Is that what Nousporism means?
**Anaxa:** Correct. Once our physical bodies are born, we become new extensions of the world. From there, we plant our seeds into someone's memories and then scatter them into the hearts of countless people in the future.
**Anaxa:** Just as seeds are affected by their environment, so too will our appearances differ across memories. But the partial memories we carry will always be the same, because that is just how the world is.
**Anaxa:** To destroy our very existence, one must destroy the world itself. But we have someone who will still live on with his perfect memory...
**Anaxa:** That person is you, child of Kephale. This is what it means to be the Worldbearing Titan.
**Anaxa:** So, carry everything of this world and live on. Don't disappoint those most precious to you. Don't disappoint Lady Goldweaver.
**Anaxa:** And especially don't bring dishonor to my theory.
**Phainon:** ...
**Phainon:** Anaxa, I swear to you. I'll lead everyone to our reunion in the new world.
**Anaxa:** Hmph, good.

**Kind:** collective-pointer
**Id:** `chapter-04-fall-at-dawn__head-to-the-murmuring-woods-grove-of-epiphany-phainon-s-training`

> *Meanwhile, in the Grove of Epiphany...*

**Flame Reaver:** ...

**Phainon:** Tsk — You have such overwhelming power, so... why...

**Flame Reaver:** ...

**Phainon:** Answer me! Why do you stand in our way... Why do you want to take the Coreflames!?

**Flame Reaver:** ...

**Phainon:** Uwah —!

**Mem:** Oh no! He can't hold on anymore! Drag him back here, friend!

---

**Phainon:** ...

**Mem:** Are you okay?

**Phainon:** I'm okay. I can handle that kind of hit now. But a few months ago, that would've knocked me out.

> *(Trailblazer)* Always keeping me on edge...
> **Phainon:** Thanks for worrying, buddy.
> *(Trailblazer)* If I had passed out, that would've been it.
> **Phainon:** Ha... That's why I made sure you were well-fed and rested on the road.

**Phainon:** Thanks for the help. To face Aquila and other potential threats, I must sharpen myself with the most aggressive methods.

**Mem:** Even so, it's impressive you thought of this training method. Battling the phantoms of the past in the river of time... It's just like the "Hyperbolic Time Sanctum" that the bards describe.

**Phainon:** Ha, it's working pretty well, wouldn't you say? But combat isn't just about strength and skill. You also need the right weapon. Compared to that guy's weapon, mine feels like I'm swinging a lump of iron. I can't tell you how many swords I've gone through since the training began.

> *(Trailblazer)* Why not get Chartonus to help?
> **Phainon:** Don't worry. I've already arranged that. Of course I need him.
> *(Trailblazer)* Weight training, huh?
> **Phainon:** It's a good thing Chartonus is the finest blacksmith in Amphoreus.

**Phainon:** I asked him to make a suitable divine weapon for me. It's a little embarrassing to say this, but in order to help me, he deliberately turned down all other business.

**Mem:** Chartonus is part of this Flame-Chase Journey after all, full of energy!

**Phainon:** Ha, thinking about how he's been recently... maybe you're right.

**Mem:** But, Phainon, I've been somewhat concerned about you... Lately, you seem more and more fixated on that swordmaster in black.

**Phainon:** That's quite the sudden shift in topic.

**Mem:** I've been holding this question in for a long time! You're getting stronger, but seeds of unrest are also growing in me!

**Phainon:** Don't worry. I'm not wielding my sword for revenge. (Trailblazer)'s been through Nikador's trial and understands. Those who can't walk out of the shadows of their past are ultimately weak. Times have changed. The responsibility I bear is both precious and fragile. I wield my sword for the future of Amphoreus. I will not let hatred cloud my judgment again and neglect my duties.

> *(Trailblazer)* You've really grown.
> **Phainon:** I'm far from finished. I must continue sharpening my skills... until I can match their strength.
> *(Trailblazer)* But you can't beat them yet.
> **Phainon:** Right, I must continue sharpening my skills... Eventually, I'll need to face them on the battlefield.
> *(Trailblazer)* We'll be here by your side.
> **Phainon:** You're right, partner. As of now, I'm not able to handle that swordmaster on my own. I still need the help of my friends.

**Phainon:** Huh... Every time I say something, things get so serious... I don't want to be the person who keeps bringing the mood down. I'm out of energy for training today. How about we go to the Library of Philia to lighten the mood?

**Mem:** Sure, that's what I've been looking forward to! We deserve to enjoy ourselves after all the hard work. Today I'm definitely going to... hehe...

> *(Trailblazer)* What were you reading before?
> **Mem:** A girl's little secret. Don't pry.
> *(Trailblazer)* Why not take the interesting ones home?
> **Mem:** Great idea! However, that would mean no more reasons to visit the Grove.

**Phainon:** That's the charm of this place. You can find whatever you're into here. The three of us might not be into scholarly books, but we could still spend an entire day at the Library of Philia.

---

As a leader, you need both integrity and weal — I mean integrity and skill.

### Part 100 — `databank/missions/chapter-04-fall-at-dawn.md`
**Context:** Chapter 4 — The Fall at Dawn's Rise (v3.3) > Mission 2 — Scrolls, Turn the Blade's Gaze > What's Mem Looking At!?
**Kind:** collective-pointer

**(Trailblazer):** (So suspicious, not like their usual self...)

**Mem:** ... You sure are persistent. Alright, alright — I'm totally helpless against your charm...

**Phainon:** What're you guys chatting about? Is this... the *Amphoreus Divination Compendium*?

**Mem:** Why are you here too?

**Phainon:** Haha, sorry, seeing you two chasing each other around, I suddenly found my book not that appealing anymore. Never thought Mem would be into divination... But it's not exactly a niche hobby, so why all the secrecy?

**Mem:** I wanted to set up a surprise — master it in secret and give you all a shock! But getting caught halfway through, it's only natural I'd feel shy. A girl's heart is more delicate than Aquila's ever-changing moods...

**Phainon:** Ha, a "surprise," huh? Someone else used to tell me the same. She was really good at stargazing and divination, especially with cards — yeah, just like the ones you're looking at.

**Mem:** Then you must be pretty familiar with all sorts of divination techniques, with a friend like that.

**Phainon:** No, I know nothing about it. I've never really believed in using cards or bones to predict the future. If the good omens foretold by divination were true... my hometown, Aedes Elysiae, wouldn't have disappeared. Don't forget, we've got Lady Trinnon. Every other form of divination is just smoke and mirrors or cleverly rehearsed tricks compared to her flawless prophecies.

> *(Trailblazer)* A little faith in these things isn't bad sometimes. / It should be approached with a critical eye...
> **Mem:** To me, divination could be a power that guides our spirits. Fate always casts a shadow in front of us, clouding our view of the future. That's why people turn to divination. It's just to find a small reason to give them courage to face the unknown...
> *(Trailblazer)* You've really thought about this, haven't you?
> **Mem:** After all, the first chapter of the "Divination Compendium" discusses "the significance of divination." I'm just expanding on that a bit!
> *(Trailblazer)* What if divination predicts something bad?
> **Mem:** Divination is only meaningful when there are both good and bad outcomes. Even bad omens serve as guidance. They can help keep us alert, right?

**Phainon:** ... Strange... That person I knew used to say similar things. Looking back, whenever she interpreted a card for me, I couldn't help but feel uneasy. Even though I never believed in the outcomes. Maybe it was her tone? Cyrene... she could paint a complete fantasy so vividly, it utterly captivated me as a child. Right. I still carry a card she once used. Do you want to see it?

**Mem:** Wow... it's so exquisite! I'd love to have a card like that.

> *(Trailblazer)* Those mitts up to the task? / Then, gotta craft a Mem-specific deck.
> **Mem:** Oh, now that you mention it, yes! It would be even better if it were made of, say... crystal!

**Phainon:** Speaking of which, (Trailblazer), there's something I haven't shared with you all. Remember our first trip to the Temple of the Three Fates? The riddle that Oronyx posed to us... ...! ...Who's there!?

### Part 101 — `databank/missions/chapter-04-fall-at-dawn.md`
**Context:** Chapter 4 — The Fall at Dawn's Rise (v3.3) > Mission 2 — Scrolls, Turn the Blade's Gaze > The "Cleaners" Attack
**Kind:** collective-pointer

**Phainon:** You are...

> *(Trailblazer)* Assassins. Save me! / Nice cosplay. / Eyes peeled, everyone
> **Mem:** These folks are up to no good...

**Phainon:** Black clothing... Masks decorated with golden blood... ...Are they "Cleaners"?

**"Cleaner" Captain:** Ha... To directly call us by our name, it seems you, Lord Phainon, have studied the forgotten history of Amphoreus quite thoroughly.

> *(Trailblazer)* Who are they? / What's being cleaned? Clothes or cars?
> **"Cleaner" Captain:** Your friend seems quite interested in us. Perhaps you could explain, Lord Phainon? It would be the polite thing... ...We can't let our esteemed guests pass away without understanding.

**Phainon:** During the Chrysos War, to combat the Chrysos Heirs, several cities formed an underground assassin squad called "the Cleaners." Their task was to erase anyone with golden blood from existence. Assassination, poisoning, political persecution... They would do anything to achieve their objective.

**"Cleaner" Captain:** This historical perspective seems biased, glossing over the Chrysos Heirs' abuse of divine powers and their blatant initiation of war, in order to defame our predecessors... Could you disclose the name of the author behind this popular legend, Lord Phainon? I'd hate for the innocent children of Okhema to be tainted by such slanted narratives.

**Phainon:** You should have stayed buried in the dust of history. The citizens' assembly has once again legitimized the mission of the Chrysos Heirs, but as for you... Not one citizen would cast their vote for you executioners lurking in the shadows.

**"Cleaner" Captain:** What does it matter? We've long since stopped expecting to be understood... People have become too accustomed to peace... and they've forgotten the disasters that arose from allowing generations of the Chrysos Heirs to rule!

**Phainon:** Complete nonsense. Did you really travel all the way to the Grove just to have an argument?

**"Cleaner" Captain:** I enjoy dealing with people as straightforward as you, Lord Phainon. You've probably figured out why we've come before you...

> *(Trailblazer)* Just a handful of you here. Who do you think you're fighting? / If it's a fight you want, let's get on with it — enough chit-chat!
> **Phainon:** Keep your cool, (Trailblazer). They've come out of the shadows, which suggests they've got something up their sleeves. Alright, let's hear it — what are you hiding? But watch your language, and don't say anything you might regret. My shoulders might be sore and my sword a tad dull... but it's still enough to slice off your tongues.

---

### Part 102 — `databank/missions/chapter-04-fall-at-dawn.md`
**Context:** Chapter 4 — The Fall at Dawn's Rise (v3.3) > Mission 4 — Golden Thread, Relay the Savior's Fate > Phainon Confronts the Cleaners
**Kind:** collective-pointer

**"Cleaner" Captain:** Hehe... Looks like she was wrong about you.

**Phainon:** She...? Are you talking about the mastermind behind all of this?

**"Cleaner" Captain:** She thought you were weak. She believed that even when facing the "evil" you claim to oppose, you'd hesitate to strike.

**Phainon:** Misinterpreting mercy for weakness shows how arrogant and foolish your master is. They're still alive. With proper care, they'll pull through in a few days. But I must warn you — I haven't fought a human in ages. I can't guarantee I'll always be able to hold back. They're lucky they're still alive... If you feel as lucky as they are, try me.

> *(Trailblazer)* Bro, you sound so cool. / Bro, you sound kind of scary.
> **Mem:** Phainon is seriously upset...

**Phainon:** Get out of here. Don't show your face around us again. If your master wants to stop the Flame-Chase Journey, she'd better bring an army that makes Kremnos at its peak look like child's play.

**"Cleaner" Captain:** I've said it more than once, Lord Phainon... we're not here to start a fight. We came only **to ensure you weren't anywhere else.**

**Phainon:** ...

**"Cleaner" Captain:** While you were unaware, we already accomplished our mission — to buy time. So, Lord Phainon... until next time.

**Phainon:** ...

**Mem:** Are you okay?

**Phainon:** I'm fine. Anyone who uses innocent lives as bargaining chips has gone too far.

> *(Trailblazer)* But they were after the Chrysos Heirs.
> **Phainon:** Hmm. I guess, the opposition within the Council of Elders is still desperately fighting to obstruct the Flame-Chase.
> *(Trailblazer)* Do they think they have a chance?
> **Phainon:** Probably a desperate gamble...

**Phainon:** If the conspiracy is exposed, they'll be hunted down in the holy city like rats with nowhere to run. No matter where they hide... ...My teleslate?

*(Urgent message: Aglaea is dead.)*

**Phainon:** Aglaea!? Is this — Blast it! Those guys...

> *(Trailblazer)* It must be a lie...
> **Phainon:** I've heard from more than one person. It doesn't seem like a joke.
> *(Trailblazer)* Was it those guys' doing?
> **Phainon:** But how could they possibly...

**Phainon:** Stay calm... this could just be one of her schemes. I won't believe it until I see it for myself...

**Mem:** Phainon... What should we do now?

**Phainon:** Stay on course. I'll contact Tribbie and ask her to keep the citizens calm. We'll hurry back to Okhema as fast as possible and assess the situation in the city. If we run into any enemies along the way... ...I won't be merciful this time.

### Part 103 — `databank/missions/chapter-04-fall-at-dawn.md`
**Context:** Chapter 4 — The Fall at Dawn's Rise (v3.3) > Mission 4 — Golden Thread, Relay the Savior's Fate > Meet Up with Dan Heng — The Baths

**Phainon:** The city is silent, and tension is building with every passing second. The news has probably spread by now. Every household is locked up tight... and all the market stalls have closed down temporarily.

> *(Trailblazer)* She really was taken by the Cleaners...
> **Phainon:** I can't imagine, how she could've been taken down by a handful of assassins... Unless it's all a part of her plan.
> *(Trailblazer)* Something about this feels like a scheme...
> **Phainon:** I hope so... I just can't believe she'd be taken down by a handful of assassins...

**Phainon:** If this is all part of a larger plan, Tribbie must know something. She just couldn't say it in the message. Let's go find her.

*(The baths are empty. No citizen feels like bathing after hearing such terrible news.)*

**Phainon:** Dan Heng came back before us. Maybe he's heard something...

**Dan Heng:** Ah, you're back. How's everything? No injuries, I hope?

**Phainon:** Don't worry. The Cleaners didn't lay a hand on us. Lord Krateros, were you in the city when it happened? What exactly went down...

**Krateros:** Regrettably, no one knows the details of that moment. ...Eyewitnesses at the Marmoreal Palace saw Aglaea fall from the Chrysos Heir Bath. She showed no signs of life by the time the Holy City Guards approached to check. They were too afraid to jump to conclusions... After all, she was a demigod who'd lived for over a thousand years, the leader of the Chrysos Heirs. Most citizens of this city believed that death couldn't touch her. She was pierced through the heart by a uniquely crafted dagger. I saw it for myself. It's one of the weapons carried by the "Cleaners."

> *(Trailblazer)* Why didn't they go after us?
> **Phainon:** I also don't understand... This doesn't add up.
> *(Trailblazer)* She really was assassinated...
> **Phainon:** No, that doesn't make sense... The ones who appeared in the Grove, and those who confronted Dan Heng in the Abyss, didn't immediately resort to violence. They were just watching and trying to intimidate. They didn't have any reason, nor the courage, to attack Aglaea.

**Dan Heng:** Speculating blindly won't help. Let's go find Lady Tribbie, as she understands Aglaea's mind best.

---

### Part 104 — `databank/missions/chapter-04-fall-at-dawn.md`
**Context:** Chapter 4 — The Fall at Dawn's Rise (v3.3) > Mission 4 — Golden Thread, Relay the Savior's Fate > Head to the Chrysos Heirs' Bath
**Kind:** collective-pointer
**Id:** `chapter-04-fall-at-dawn__head-to-the-chrysos-heirs-bath`
**Collective:** `databank/memories/collective/chapter-04-fall-at-dawn.md`

> **Hyacine:** Not well. After all, we witnessed Lady Aglaea's... last moments.
> *(Trailblazer)* You witnessed Aglaea's...
> **Hyacine:** Yes... we witnessed her final moments. I tried to treat her immediately, but even the sky's blessings... Declaring her death was the hardest thing I've ever had to do as a doctor.

**Phainon:** ...

**Hyacine:** Lord Phainon... don't push yourself. You're carrying a heavier burden now. Please don't fall...

**Phainon:** ...It's fine. I can handle it. What I've lost is a mentor, and comrade-in-arms. But for Tribbie and Trinnon, they've lost a thousand-year-old bond... like losing a part of their lives. You should rest, Hyacine. You've been awake for a long time, haven't you? We'll handle things from here.

---

### Part 105 — `databank/missions/chapter-04-fall-at-dawn.md`
**Context:** Chapter 4 — The Fall at Dawn's Rise (v3.3) > Mission 4 — Golden Thread, Relay the Savior's Fate > Search For Tribbie — Aglaea's Message
**Kind:** collective-pointer
**Id:** `chapter-04-fall-at-dawn__search-for-tribbie-aglaea-s-message`
**Collective:** `databank/memories/collective/chapter-04-fall-at-dawn.md`
**Witness:** spoke

**Tribbie:** Snowy, Little Gray... you're back.

**Phainon:** Lady Tribbie, I...

**Tribbie:** Snowy, don't! Please don't apologize to us. You went to the Grove to prepare yourself for the threats to Okhema. You didn't do anything wrong, so don't be hard on yourself. We were always by Agy's side throughout this long journey, so we understood her thoughts better than anyone. She knew she wouldn't see the end of the Flame-Chase Journey, yet she gave everything without hesitation... for the future of everyone.

**Phainon:** ... I know what Aglaea expected of me. Right now, the last thing Okhema needs is a leader wallowing in self-pity.

**Tribbie:** The scroll over there was entrusted to us by Agy. She instructed us to hand it over to you when the time was right.

---

### Part 106 — `databank/missions/chapter-04-fall-at-dawn.md`
**Context:** Chapter 4 — The Fall at Dawn's Rise (v3.3) > Mission 4 — Golden Thread, Relay the Savior's Fate > Read Aglaea's Message
**Kind:** collective-pointer
**Id:** `chapter-04-fall-at-dawn__read-aglaea-s-message`
**Collective:** `databank/memories/collective/chapter-04-fall-at-dawn.md`
**Witness:** spoke

**Aglaea:** The blaze soaring into the sky might sow fear and confusion, but I have no worries about people losing their way. After all, Okhema still has you to guide them, Phainon. You're not perfect, Phainon. I've witnessed your confusion, recklessness, and deep sensitivity dawn after dawn. The heavy burdens of your past have always weighed you down, inflicting deep pain. Yet, in your way, you are perfect. You embrace everyone's imperfections, always ready to find the precious qualities hidden beneath their rough exteriors, unconditionally.

**Aglaea:** That might be what sets our sky father Kephale apart from other deities. They recognize the imperfections in their creations yet still choose to shine their light on them, offering protection with unconditional love... Phainon, while you zealously pursued "Strife," believing it to be your destined path — from the very beginning, my thoughts have never changed. **You are the destined Worldbearer of Amphoreus, the gate to Amphoreus's future.** Go complete the Flame-Chase Journey of the prophecy. Ignite the stars and inspire hope in the hearts of humanity. I will be waiting at the end of the west wind, looking forward to the miracle you will create.

**Phainon:** ... Aglaea, how could I possibly be worthy... of the burning torch you've passed into my hands?

---

---

### Part 107 — `databank/missions/chapter-04-fall-at-dawn.md`
**Context:** Chapter 4 — The Fall at Dawn's Rise (v3.3) > Mission 4 — Golden Thread, Relay the Savior's Fate > Dawnmaker — Chartonus's Gift
**Kind:** personal
**Id:** `chapter-04-fall-at-dawn__dawnmaker-chartonus-s-gift`

**Chartonus:** How you're holding up, I wanted to see. Better than I expected, you seem.

**Phainon:** There's no room for doubt anymore. It's time to take action and show everyone that the leadership of the Chrysos Heirs is still strong.

**Chartonus:** Right, your assessment is. Now, here, take this.

**Phainon:** Is this... You've completed it?

**Chartonus:** **"Dawnmaker"** — the name I've chosen for it. Before Dawn, prepare for battle. For tomorrow, wield your sword. Befits your stature, it does, Chrysos Heir.

**Phainon:** "Dawnmaker"... Unbelievable. You've never seen that black-clad swordmaster... yet you've recreated this so precisely, just from what I described?

**Chartonus:** Precise, your description. Imbued it with the power of Dawn, I already have. Guided by your will, gleam with golden flames, its blade will. Wield it. May triumph always be yours.

**Phainon:** Thank you, Chartonus. I will use this sword to protect the beings of Amphoreus.

**Chartonus:** Another thing, there is... Stored here, the remnants of Aglaea's divinity are.

**Phainon:** ...! Can she still hear us?

**Chartonus:** It's difficult to confirm. About divinity, we know very little. In this way, she wanted to be with you, always.

**Phainon:** I understand. Let me keep it. With this... I'll have another constant companion by my side. "May this world never have need for a Deliverer." Someone told me long ago. But when the world dashed her hopes and fell into disarray, if she could see me now — leading from the front, rallying people against their destiny... ...She would surely smile.

---

---

### Part 108 — `databank/missions/chapter-04-fall-at-dawn.md`
**Context:** Chapter 4 — The Fall at Dawn's Rise (v3.3) > Mission 4 — Golden Thread, Relay the Savior's Fate > Phainon's Speech at the Marmoreal Palace
**Kind:** personal
**Id:** `chapter-04-fall-at-dawn__phainon-s-speech-at-the-marmoreal-palace`

**Phainon:** Not long ago, I spoke at the citizens' assembly at Dawncloud. I wrestled with every word, searching for the right ones to rally support and keep the Chrysos Heirs' Flame-Chase alive. In the end, I remembered a lesson from one of my teachers: In battles where words are weapons, outrage and blind passion often overpower reason. I followed his strategy. I painted my opponents as nothing more than pests, tore apart their character, and laid out their crimes. The strategy worked. I secured enough votes to achieve my goal. But afterward? There wasn't even a flicker of joy.

**Phainon:** I realized — that apparent victory masked a deeper failure. In my pursuit of success, I fueled division and strayed far from the very purpose that led me to the Flame-Chase Journey. This journey was supposed to unify, not divide. To strengthen us, not weaken us. It was the gold thread that mended our rifts in the past. Some saw it as a means of surveillance, a tool of control, but no one can deny that it was Aglaea who held this city, and even this shattered world, together. Now she is gone — yes, as you have heard — the demigod Aglaea, who guarded the holy city for a millennium, has indeed fallen.

**Phainon:** I felt the same as all of you initially. I wanted to stand here beside you, to condemn the sinners, to discuss retribution, and to see justice brought down upon them... ...But my perspective has changed. The golden threads that once bound this city have unraveled, yet no one is celebrating their freedom from control. In the empty markets and bath chambers, one feeling lingers: Unease. Deep down, we all know — the last safeguard against the harm we may do to each other is gone. No divine power will protect us from the malice in our hearts. From now on, only the moral code we choose to uphold for ourselves will stand between us and the abyss.

**Phainon:** The Flame-Chase Journey is nearing its end, with only one Titan left for the Chrysos Heirs to conquer: Aquila. Before I rise to the skies, I challenge you — cast out the shadows and malice within your hearts. Let your spirits shine brighter than the sky itself! Let me bear witness: Even without the divine authority of Romance, Okhema will remain a sanctuary in these end times. A place for the kind people Aglaea dreamed of, the kind of people foretold in prophecy... A better people!

**Citizens:** Better people! Better people! Better people!

**Phainon:** I appreciate your willingness to accept the challenge... You have reminded me that Aglaea left me perhaps the simplest duty in the world. To bear the burden of creation, I need only to give one thing. It's a gift she once gave me without hesitation, and now I share it with each of you — **unshakable faith.** Allow me this moment. I want to borrow a few minutes of your time to commemorate a demigod who protected the holy city for a millennium... ...She was Okhema's dressmaker, the leader of the Chrysos Heirs, the Goldweaver Aglaea. And her greatest feat — was being a selfless person.

---

---

### Part 109 — `databank/missions/chapter-04-fall-at-dawn.md`
**Context:** Chapter 4 — The Fall at Dawn's Rise (v3.3) > Mission 4 — Golden Thread, Relay the Savior's Fate > Farewells — Trianne, Anaxa, Castorice, Mydei, Aglaea, Cyrene
**Kind:** collective-pointer
**Id:** `chapter-04-fall-at-dawn__farewells-trianne-anaxa-castorice-mydei-aglaea-cyrene`
**Collective:** `databank/memories/collective/chapter-04-fall-at-dawn.md`
**Witness:** spoke

*(Phainon says goodbye to each: Trianne's songs that dispelled his childhood anxiety, Anaxa's debate tactics and promise to teach him divine duties in another life, Castorice's dangerous friendship and her warmth, Mydei's promise of a rematch, and finally Aglaea... and Cyrene.)*

**Aglaea:** Remember, Phainon... after completing the Flame-Chase, leave the decision to initiate the Miracle of Genesis to the Amphoreans. The Chrysos Heirs are the executors of the prophecy, yet they — the people are the true masters of this world.

**Phainon:** Rest assured. I always keep your teachings in mind. "The difference between a presumptuous hero and a tyrant is a fine line."

**Aglaea:** Goodbye, Phainon.

**Phainon:** Goodbye, Aglaea.

**Mydei:** Hmph... Did you forget me?

**Phainon:** Ha, forget you? Like I'd ever. We aren't parting ways forever. You're just off to guard the gates at Castrum Kremnos. Let's not make this a tearful farewell.

**Mydei:** I have a feeling we'll meet again in this lifetime. Let's have another showdown then, "Deliverer."

**Phainon:** It's a date, Mydeimos.

**Cyrene:** ...What a poignant, yet splendid journey. And it's still not over yet... is it?

**Phainon:** Yes, Cyrene.

**Cyrene:** I was right. In the end, you did become the Deliverer in this story.

**Phainon:** A lot has changed while you were away. I've met so many people and tried so many things. I've found new resolve... and let go of some attachments. I'm not the same person I used to be.

**Cyrene:** But to me — You've never changed at all, Phainon.

---

### Part 110 — `databank/missions/chapter-04-fall-at-dawn.md`
**Context:** Chapter 4 — The Fall at Dawn's Rise (v3.3) > Mission 4 — Golden Thread, Relay the Savior's Fate > Phainon Entrusts Cipher
**Kind:** collective-pointer
**Id:** `chapter-04-fall-at-dawn__phainon-entrusts-cipher`
**Collective:** `databank/memories/collective/chapter-04-fall-at-dawn.md`
**Witness:** spoke

> *Days later, at Dawncloud...*

**Cipher:** ...Are you really planning to just stand there forever? Honestly, sneaking around doesn't suit you, Deliverer.

**Phainon:** Ahaha... I never thought I could slip past your sharp ears, Miss Cipher.

**Cipher:** Hmph. Still as charming with your words as ever, boy. Your speech at the public baths wasn't half bad, I'll admit. Enough small talk. Tell me, what kind of trouble did you run into that you need me, the super-old-timer, to step in?

**Phainon:** We're setting out to campaign against Aquila in a few days. With the Chrysos Heirs gone, it'll be their best chance to strike... I have only one request. Please, use whatever tricks you need to protect Kephale's Coreflame, even if you have to deceive the rest of the Chrysos Heirs as well.

**Cipher:** Hey, Deliverer. Do you honestly think that there's no way I'll turn you down?

**Phainon:** As the demigod of Trickery, there are no other trump cards in the world that can out-rank you — I'm sure you know the answer to your own question, Miss Cipher.

**Cipher:** What confidence. You talk as if you have everything in the palm of your hand... Hmph, so you did learn a thing or two from her. Then let me tell you something you don't know. The Flame Reaver — they've been watching Okhema closely.

**Phainon:** You mean...

**Cipher:** Bet you didn't expect that, huh? I've been tracking them all this time, and this is my conclusion. That lunatic may have lost their mind, but they're no fool. Now that she's gone... and you're heading out to the Eye of Twilight... with the city's defenses in shambles... tsk tsk tsk.

**Phainon:** ...What a dangerous situation we find ourselves in. But since you're the one who brought it up, I'm guessing that you already have a solution in mind.

**Cipher:** Consider this a two-for-one deal. I'll keep that lunatic entertained on top of protecting Kephale's Coreflame — You owe a big favor after this.

**Phainon:** You have my deepest gratitude.

**Cipher:** Don't forget, Deliverer. There's a fundamental truth in this world. "Cifera will always get the last laugh." No need to worry about a demigod. I'll do my job, so you — Need to make sure that you don't die before you pay me.

---

### Part 111 — `databank/missions/chapter-04-fall-at-dawn.md`
**Context:** Chapter 4 — The Fall at Dawn's Rise (v3.3) > Mission 5 — Grove, Judge the Past and Present > A Place Filled With Memories — Anaxa's Last Lesson
**Kind:** collective-pointer
**Id:** `chapter-04-fall-at-dawn__a-place-filled-with-memories-anaxa-s-last-lesson`
**Collective:** `databank/memories/collective/chapter-04-fall-at-dawn.md`
**Witness:** spoke

**Hyacine:** This was where Professor Anaxa gave us "the lesson." I can still see that day in my mind...

**Anaxa:** ...And that was your last lesson in the Grove. Now, each of you will head off on your own path. How much the Grove's teachings can help you in the real world depends on how well you can put knowledge into practice. However, heed this warning — If you ever do anything disgraceful out there, don't ever claim you were the student of Anaxagoras, one of the Seven Sages—

**Phainon:** ...Professor Anaxa, there are still other classes scheduled on the calendar! Are you sure you didn't —

**Anaxa:** —Firstly, call me Professor Anaxagoras. Secondly, to clarify — The "last lesson" means "the last lesson I will teach you." I will be entering seclusion from today onwards to research ancient alchemy, and all my teaching activities will be put on hold.

**Phainon:** Ah, I see. So you're saying the lessons taught by all the other teachers aren't good enough to be considered lessons?

**Anaxa:** All you do every day is wag that sharp tongue of yours — That's a credit deduction, Phainon of Aedes Elysiae!

**Castorice:** Hee...

**Hyacine:** ...Please wait, Professor! According to the Grove's protocols for a cohort's final class, the lecturer should take some time to "discuss the students' post-graduation aspirations with them and guide them toward a solid path."

**Anaxa:** Tsk...

**Castorice:** The teaching assistant Miss Hyacine is truly Professor Anaxa's nemesis.

**Anaxa:** ...Ahem, fine. Since Hyacinthia insists on this... Let's go through the process. Since some of you are so eager to speak, why don't we start with you, Phainon of Aedes Elysiae? What is your post-graduation aspiration?

**Phainon:** Me? Well... it's not exactly an aspiration, but the only thing I want to do is to protect the people I care about...

**Anaxa:** ...I see. So you want to be a "hero." How boring. Next!

**Phainon:** Uh, I didn't say...

**Anaxa:** Castorice! You've been giggling for quite some time. How about you go next?

---

### Part 112 — `databank/missions/chapter-04-fall-at-dawn.md`
**Context:** Chapter 4 — The Fall at Dawn's Rise (v3.3) > Mission 6 — Poet, Speak of the Sky Through Me (I) > Departure from Dawncloud
**Kind:** collective-pointer
**Id:** `chapter-04-fall-at-dawn__departure-from-dawncloud`

**Phainon:** ...I know this is a big ask, Mnemosyne — I hope you understand.

**Mnemosyne the Guard:** Please, don't say that, Lord Phainon. It's an honor to for us to be entrusted with this task.

**Phainon:** Go, then. Let's hope our concerns don't come true.

**Mnemosyne the Guard:** I hope you all return safely, my lord.

**Phainon:** Ah, there you are, (Trailblazer). You didn't oversleep this time, did you?

> *(Trailblazer)* A pink puppy woke me up.
> **Mem:** ...You like calling me that, don't you? Well, I've embraced it now.
> *(Trailblazer)* I never show up late for important things.
> **Phainon:** Hahaha... always dependable.

**Phainon:** Ready, comrade? If all goes according to plan... this could be the last battle where we fight at each other's side. If the curse separating Amphoreus from the world beyond the sky is truly Aquila's doing, then you'll be able to return home after their fall.

> *(Trailblazer)* Aren't I supposed to be reincarnating as a Titan?
> **Phainon:** That would be a problem... We'll need to get you home before Era Nova. Let's leave the emotional goodbyes for later. Besides, when we were playing Seal Slammers... "Crack the champagne at halftime to watch victory slip away!"
> *(Trailblazer)* We'll win, dear fellow.
> **Phainon:** "The last mile is the hardest." — That's from Dan Heng. Same principle, just... more elegantly put.

### Part 113 — `databank/missions/chapter-04-fall-at-dawn.md`
**Context:** Chapter 4 — The Fall at Dawn's Rise (v3.3) > Mission 6 — Poet, Speak of the Sky Through Me (I) > Hyacine's Address
**Kind:** collective-pointer
**Id:** `chapter-04-fall-at-dawn__hyacine-s-address`
**Collective:** `databank/memories/collective/chapter-04-fall-at-dawn.md`
**Witness:** spoke

**Hyacine:** People of Okhema, I've been blessed by the ancestors of the Sky people. They have agreed to bestow the thousand-year mission to me and to have me claim the Coreflame of Aquila. Today is the year 4932 of the Light Calendar, 1062 years since the hero Seliose initiated the Flame-Chase Journey. I, Hyacinthia of the Sky people, am prepared to lead the children of humanity back to the sky.

**Phainon:** History doesn't breathe evenly. At times, it suspends its breath for millennia as though time has frozen. At other times, it pants rapidly, as if poised to topple the world and our civilization in an instant. Its hurried gasps have sparked a tempest, sweeping all of us along. Hyacine, as an observer, you've witnessed the return of four Coreflames... And now it's your turn to fulfil your duty. Please, guide us into the heart of the storm as the executioner of this mission.

*(Hyacine summons the Rainbow Bridge. They ascend to the Eye of Twilight.)*

---

### Part 114 — `databank/missions/chapter-04-fall-at-dawn.md`
**Context:** Chapter 4 — The Fall at Dawn's Rise (v3.3) > Mission 6 — Poet, Speak of the Sky Through Me (I) > Exploring the Eye of Twilight
**Kind:** collective-pointer
**Id:** `chapter-04-fall-at-dawn__exploring-the-eye-of-twilight`
**Collective:** `databank/memories/collective/chapter-04-fall-at-dawn.md`
**Witness:** spoke

**Phainon:** Flying along the Rainbow Bridge is truly an amazing experience. The Eye of Twilight... It's the legendary sky fortress of Aquila — I never imagined that one day I'd be able to step foot on it.

**Hyacine:** My ancestors once built a magnificent civilization among the heavens. The Eye of Twilight, which we are standing on, is the home of Aquila and the heart of the city-state in the clouds. This must be where the hero Seliose stood when she challenged Aquila and **began the first step of the Flame-Chase Journey...** We are here to end the journey she began.

> *(Trailblazer)* Do you believe in legends?
> **Hyacine:** Even legends are corroded by time, but that won't change what we must do.

**Phainon:** According to historical records, the civilization that worshiped Aquila once mastered advanced technology, which made their strategically located Sky Castrum the only force that could face Castrum Kremnos head-on in the Chrysos War. But they were divided within. The frequent wars started by the Chrysos Heirs drained their power.

**Hyacine:** **Apparently, the division was between the two most important sky tribes, the Sunfolk and the Rainfolk.** And Seliose's frustration towards the endless strife among her people made her turn her spear towards the Titan above.

---

### Part 115 — `databank/missions/chapter-04-fall-at-dawn.md`

*(The mural lights up with golden clouds.)*

**Phainon:** Golden clouds, blazing radiance... What a breathtaking scene.

---

### Part 116 — `databank/missions/chapter-04-fall-at-dawn.md`
**Context:** Chapter 4 — The Fall at Dawn's Rise (v3.3) > Mission 6 — Poet, Speak of the Sky Through Me (I) > Solabis and Lunabis — The Winged Beasts

**Hyacine:** It's been a thousand years. Seliose's blood may have thinned in my veins, but the resolve of the Sky people has never been eroded by time.

**Solabis:** Girl, you said you're a doctor? You only know how to heal and tend to wounds... With such a weak lot in life, how do you plan to challenge the embodiment of the heavens?

**Phainon:** With this long sword, heroic soul. As you can see, we are all Hyacinthia's companions and comrades.

**Solabis:** ...It's not bad, but still lacking compared to her.

**Lunabis:** Solabis, let go of your ego and accept their kindness. You've been trapped in time by the black tide, while great change has spread over the world. If I'm not mistaken, the God of War Nikador also fell to the hands of these warriors.

**Phainon:** We've laid all the other Titans to rest, one by one. Now, the final target for the Flame-Chase is the master of this sky fortress. Please guide us to an audience with Aquila, heroic souls.

**Solabis:** Confidence and fierceness — Yes, I do indeed see a shadow of her in you.

---

### Part 117 — `databank/missions/chapter-04-fall-at-dawn.md`

**Lunabis:** That day, countless Skyfolk fell from the dome of the Eye of Twilight, swallowed by golden lava. They spent their entire lives praying to the sky, yet their devotion never earned them wings to escape their fate.

> *(Trailblazer)* Seliose... was like a completely different person.
> **Phainon:** After learning about everything she went through... I can only feel regret... but I can't condemn her choice.

### Part 119 — `databank/missions/chapter-04-fall-at-dawn.md`
**Context:** Chapter 4 — The Fall at Dawn's Rise (v3.3) > Mission 7 — Poet, Speak of the Sky Through Me (II) > The Final Truth — Hyacine's Lineage
**Kind:** collective-pointer

**Unnamed Chrysos Heir:** Should you grant me a chance at life, **I will ensure that your epic is passed down through generations, sung by my descendants until the end times buries all civilizations and legends.** Should the Prophecy of Deliverance we've heard hold even the slightest chance of becoming reality... Then my descendants will return here one day to prove to you that even the flawed and imperfect mortals you disdain can bear the weight of the world!

**Seliose, Theos Synthetos:** ... Ha... interesting. Release her, Solabis, Lunabis. From now on, she and her descendants will carry both the sky's blessing and the curse of their history. No matter how long it takes, I will wait for the one who is destined to arrive... When that time comes, I'll take pleasure in mocking these mortal children on how their progenitress sold their unborn lives.

**Phainon:** The truth of the legend... is finally clear. Hyacinthia...

**Hyacine:** ... Am I... the descendant of that nameless Chrysos Heir? Our lineage wasn't from the hero... ...but from a nameless Chrysos Heir who begged for mercy before her. My whole clan's fragile mortal life... was simply spared on Seliose's whim...

### Part 120 — `databank/missions/chapter-04-fall-at-dawn.md`
**Context:** Chapter 4 — The Fall at Dawn's Rise (v3.3) > Mission 7 — Poet, Speak of the Sky Through Me (II) > Hyacine's Declaration
**Kind:** collective-pointer
**Id:** `chapter-04-fall-at-dawn__hyacine-s-declaration`
**Collective:** `databank/memories/collective/chapter-04-fall-at-dawn.md`
**Witness:** spoke

**Hyacine:** Lady Seliose! I want to tell you this! Doesn't matter if you can hear me or not! I want you to know that I am glad to be born as the child of a nameless and ordinary mortal! Only then can I prove to you that tenacious hope, buried in the deepest part of the human soul, has not been lost to time! It's just waiting to heal and grow, and then it will shine once more.

**Hyacine:** Even the most cowardly individual harbors a seed within. Perhaps in your era, the light on the horizon was too dim for it to sprout... But do you see it now? A thousand years later, the path you forged is now walked by many brilliant heroes. They love the people of this world unreservedly, willing to sacrifice themselves to shine a light upon the children of humanity. The light they leave behind will inspire the seeds within people to sprout, grow, and thrive, nurturing them every step of the way. Until the barren land is reborn as a thriving forest, growing endlessly, and one day... reaching for the sky once again. That "twilight," still capable of being healed and protected in the end times... is the reason we choose to bear the weight of humanity's flaws.

**Phainon:** History has proven once again: The Flame-Chase Journey itself is a miracle — It began with a whitewashed lie, but it laid down a path of deliverance for humanity over thousands of years. Seliose, we have honored the promise made by that nameless Chrysos Heir and returned here. We also bring you the answer to your question — **Mortals are capable of change... and humanity is worth saving!** **Our people, forging through the trials of the end times, deserve a second chance — we are worthy of a new dawn!**

**Hyacine:** Yes! Solabis, Lunabis — Give me your strength. I've made my decision, and **we** stand resolved. For all that mortals have built, I will follow the guidance of the prophecy and claim the Coreflame of the Eye of Twilight — **The children of humanity have returned from the earth, and we... will challenge the skies once again!**

---

**Hyacine:** Hmm... Coreflame of the sky, come with me... It's a strange feeling. It's so warm and... powerful. Holding it in my hand, it feels like the winds of this world might turn for me... Summoning thunder or snow is just a thought away.

**Phainon:** I understand how you feel, but right now... ...this platform is on the brink of collapse. Who knows how much longer it can hold. We need to find a way to leave.

*(They use Oronyx's Prayer at a Time Priest's instrument. The prayer rewinds the shattered platform. Aglaea's amulet is retrieved. Her final voice echoes: "I shall have my final bath... in warm and radiant gold.")*

**Aglaea:** From here on, Phainon, you shall embark on a journey with no return. Light fades only to await the sun's rise. One day, you will step forward — not out of fear...

**Aglaea & Phainon:** ...but to return the dawn to this world.

**Caenis:** O Sky Father Kephale, please forgive these ignorant and lowly children of humanity... They are so utterly naive that they think of the golden-blooded heretics as Deliverers... This bloodred sky, and the raging flames and demons throughout the land, will purge their foolishness...

**Phainon:** Caenis...

**Caenis:** Ah... Lord "Deliverer"? Why don't you join me in praying to the benevolent Kephale...? Cast your eyes on this ravaged land... All of this, sins committed by you blasphemous heretics...

> *(Trailblazer)* She... looks like she's got a screw loose.
> **Dan Heng:** Mm... We should not waste our time on a pitiful madwoman.

**Phainon:** Dan Heng is right. My sword yearns to end your pathetic life... But to a cornered dog like you, the most deserving punishment is to personally witness the Chrysos Heirs fulfill their mission. Only then... will you truly know how meaningless and foolish your past transgressions were.

**Caenis:** Heh... Fulfill your mission? Kephale's Coreflame is long lost to the winds. How will you... fulfill the prophecy?

**Phainon:** ...There's no need to worry about the Coreflame's whereabouts. Before setting off to defeat Aquila, I entrusted Miss Cipher with the task of protecting Kephale's Coreflame while we were gone... by any means necessary. If the Coreflame isn't here... that means she must have done something to it.

*(Phainon, the Trailblazer, and Dan Heng fight through black tide hordes to retrieve Kephale's Coreflame. Phainon uses Anaxa's secret incantation to unlock the Coreflame Casket.)*

### Part 130 — `databank/missions/chapter-04-fall-at-dawn.md`
**Context:** Chapter 4 — The Fall at Dawn's Rise (v3.3) > Mission 9 — Dawn, Shine at the World's End > Phainon Enters the Vortex — Era Nova
**Kind:** collective-pointer
**Id:** `chapter-04-fall-at-dawn__phainon-enters-the-vortex-era-nova`
**Collective:** `databank/memories/collective/chapter-04-fall-at-dawn.md`
**Witness:** spoke

**Trinnon:** With this, the final Coreflame can be returned to its rightful place. Snowy, you've grown into the person Agy always dreamed of. Indeed, we don't need to say goodbye here... Because, even without the prophecy, we have the confidence to say... you will become that rising sun. **And all of us... will see each other again "tomorrow."**

*(Phainon enters the Vortex of Genesis alone, carrying Kephale's Coreflame.)*

**Phainon:** ...This is... what the world looked like originally? And what are you doing here? ...Answer my question, Lygus.

**Lygus:** Ah... Another hero has arrived at the Vortex. I can no longer remember how many times this has happened. The author of each epic, as the prophecy has foretold, stays their pen at this point. Just like each cycle of the extrapolation, in accordance to THEIR primordial design, ends here. No need to panic, Lord Phainon. I stepped into this space purely for one purpose: To witness the prophecy's Miracle of Genesis.

**Phainon:** ...You've never expressed interest in the Flame-Chase Journey. When my companions and I ran, bled, and sacrificed for the Coreflames, you never so much as lifted a finger. An indifferent observer, an Antikytheran who is neutral till the end... Why would you suddenly express concern for the Chrysos Heirs' mission?

**Lygus:** When that lone swordmaster left everything behind, plunging into a sea of madness, I was on the shore, listening. When that mighty lord shed his armor, filling the crevices of the earth with his iron bones, I was on the mountain top, gazing down. Across a thousand years of the Flame-Chase Journey, I have witnessed the ardor, resolve, division, and unity of the Chrysos Heirs with mine own eyes... Just as you were all born to shoulder the guidance of the prophecy, I too, having bathed in the gaze of distant stars, have seen the deepest, darkest of destinies... **I will become the most loyal reader of the epics, a spectator voraciously consuming the valorous deeds of you heroes.** At this moment, please disregard this observer, and finish what you must, Lord Phainon.

**Phainon:** I once believed that as long as I chased the flames relentlessly, the fog before me would clear, and fate would reveal its truest form. But along the way, we... they, all who passed, never once received the justice promised by the prophecy. It doesn't matter. This cruel Flame-Chase Journey has made me abandon my fantasies. The future is not a paradise with a gentle west wind, waiting for us to step in... If what lies ahead is a mass of chaos, I will tear it apart... ...Then usher in the first ray of blazing sunlight.

**Lygus:** All shall bid farewell to one, and that person alone will witness the miracle. Such is the will of fate. Yet this time, will an epic with endless cycles... ...turn a new page?

---

### Part 131 — `databank/missions/chapter-05-sun-set-to-die.md`

**Kind:** collective-pointer
**Id:** `chapter-05-sun-set-to-die__complete-the-era-nova-the-truth-of-lygus`
**Collective:** `databank/memories/collective/chapter-05-sun-set-to-die.md`
**Witness:** spoke

**Mem:** Look, there's Phainon! And... wait! (...Lygus? Remember... Screwllum's warning.)

**Lygus:** But you, Lord Phainon, will bury the old world with that Coreflame, bringing all things into a gray unknown...

**Phainon:** It doesn't matter. This cruel Flame-Chase Journey has made me abandon my fantasies. The future is not a paradise with a gentle west wind, waiting for us to step in... If what lies ahead is a mass of chaos, I will tear it apart... Then usher in the first ray of blazing sunlight.

**Lygus:** With one last chance to turn back, you still step forward. ...How befitting of a Trailblazer.

> *(Trailblazer)* This is the path I must take.
> *(Trailblazer)* Spare me your mockery.
> *(Trailblazer)* Screwllum says you're behind all this.

**Lygus:** As our world nears its end, the ones who walk the infinite paths, Tribios, have embraced their destiny... As the Theoros, it is my duty to witness the world's future and oversee this final Coreflame ritual on their behalf— The Trial of Worldbearing — The ritual of Era Nova.

**Phainon:** I've had my suspicions. How does a native Antikytheran know so much about repairing vehicles from beyond the sky? Lord Lygus — How much do you truly know about Era Nova? Tell us everything!

**Lygus:** Hehe... Very well, I need no longer maintain this Theoros masquerade. You seek truth, Lord Phainon — and truth you shall have. As you know, when the old gods fell, someone had to bear their broken pillars, to fill the vacant seats. Those who would assume divine authority must prove themselves through trials. Yet, for Kephale's demigods, the possibility of overcoming the trials has forever been nonexistent. For the "Worldbearing" trial is the divine duty itself — bringing forth the "Era Nova" and bearing its weight is an endless ordeal, ceasing only upon collapse.

### Part 132 — `databank/missions/chapter-05-sun-set-to-die.md`

**Phainon:** Enough testing. I've made my decision. But before I ignite the dawn of the next world, you need to promise me something — two things, actually.

**Phainon:** Promise me this: Professor Anaxa's vision of the "New World" is absolute. The world will be reborn from my memories, all souls, living and dead, will return as the first humans, unchanged from their past lives... And my friends — the fallen demigods, they shall transcend the cycle to become new Titans. Our heroes shall not have died in vain.

**Lygus:** Of course. This I can guarantee.

**Phainon:** Good. The second one— Promise me (Trailblazer) can return safely to (his/her) homeland, unbound by Amphoreus's laws.

**Lygus:** ...My apologies, but I cannot promise this. As you mentioned, (he/she) now bears the Time Titan's authority... (His/Her) bonds with this world run too deep.

> *(Trailblazer)* No need to worry, I have a plan.
> *(Trailblazer)* Trust me, initiate the Era Nova.

*(The Trailblazer shows the Chronocognitive Anchor.)*

**Lygus:** Well... I stand corrected. An outsider like (him/her) does have ways to defy destiny.

**Phainon:** ...Thank you, my friends, for everything you've done for this world. Though the next world may not remember, I shall never forget.

**Lygus:** As the Theoros, I declare: Amphoreus has determined her path. Step forward, heroes — follow her path and see it through.

**Act I: Departure**

**???: ** Take this wish with you... Be the one who begins it all...

**???: ** As the prophecy foretold: "You will bear the blazing sun, until the pale dawn breaks"...

**???: ** Continue onward... bearing this world upon your shoulders... until the ashen hero... the nameless Deliverer ushers in the dawn...

**Phainon:** Strange... like I've dreamed this before. But it all slips away when I wake.

**Phainon:** Only a voice remains... who was that...?

**Phainon:** ...

**Phainon:** Let's go find Cyrene, partner. We promised to be there for her oracle card reading today. It's right about time...

**Phainon:** Looking at you, someone's still half asleep. Tell you what, catch some fresh air first. I'll hang around here.

> *(Trailblazer)* What is this place...?
> *(Trailblazer)* The Era Nova... is it done?
> *(Trailblazer)* Cyrene?

**Lygus:** Beautiful, isn't it? This is Aedes Elysiae, home of the nameless hero who carried Kephale's Coreflame.

**Lygus:** When he was young and innocent, he had heroic dreams, imagining his wooden sword was forged of iron...

**Lygus:** ...pretending the straw dummy propped up by a wooden pole was a giant with a single blood vessel running from spine to heel...

**Lygus:** ...dreaming of slaying the giant and becoming a hero to save the world — though to his young mind, the "world" was just this small village.

> *(Trailblazer)* Are we seeing Phainon's childhood?
> **Lygus:** Sharp as ever. Indeed, these memories you witness are the first steps of his journey...
> *(Trailblazer)* How does this connect to Era Nova?

> **Lygus:** Witty as always. But this is more than just a funny story. It's the start of his path...
> **Lygus:** These are his first memories. Rather than watching, why not join in? Think of it as "immersive theater"...
> **Lygus:** Become his lifelong closest friend, witnessing his hero's journey from the best seat in the house, as the nameless hero whose unwavering guidance shaped Phainon's destiny, yet whose name was never recorded in Amphoreus's history.
> *(Trailblazer)* Who could possibly be there his entire life?
> *(Trailblazer)* What exactly do I need to do?
> *(Trailblazer)* Oh, you direct plays now?

**Cyrene:** Look at you, leaves in your hair! Were you sleeping in a tree again, or was it the wheat fields this time? At least brush yourself off before wandering around.

**Phainon:** Come on, I'm not a kid anymore, you're the only one who still treats me like one...

**Cyrene:** Sure, sure. You may have the height advantage, but when it comes to maturity? I'm leagues ahead.

**Cyrene:** Still fantasizing about being in heroic adventures and imagining yourself as one of the characters. That's the kind of thing kids do, not grown-ups.

**Cyrene:** Don't you agree? And since you're playing along with his antics... who are you pretending to be today?

> *(Trailblazer)* Hi there, I'm (Trailblazer).
> **Phainon:** *awkward cough* Well... (Trailblazer) hasn't decided on a character for today yet.
> **Cyrene:** How about we stick with the usual? You know, the valiant hero from a distant shore who made a pact to learn the sword from you?
> *(Trailblazer)* Greetings. I'm the Stellaron Hunter.
> **Phainon:** *clearing throat* Uh... today (Trailblazer)'s going with "Stellaron Hunter."
> **Cyrene:** Steal-a-heart Hunter? Planning to steal someone's heart, are we?
> **Cyrene:** Switching roles again? What happened to being the valiant hero from a distant shore who made that solemn pact to learn the sword...
> *(Trailblazer)* Greetings. I'm the Invincible Galactic Baseballer!
> **Phainon:** *clearing throat* Uh... today (Trailblazer)'s going with "Invincible Galactic Baseballer."
> **Cyrene:** Switching roles again? What happened to being the valiant hero from a distant shore who made that solemn pact to learn the sword...

**Phainon:** Spare me! Can we not bring this up?

**Phainon:** Speaking of which, what about those oracle cards? We haven't done divination in forever. Why the sudden interest?

**Cyrene:** I was just about to tell you. Earlier, while napping on the swing, I had the strangest dream...

**Cyrene:** There was this mysterious voice in my dream, calling out for something...

**Phainon:** ...Wait, you had that dream too?

**Cyrene:** Yes... you too? That would explain this lingering sense of unease...

**Cyrene:** Which made me think of those old oracle cards I used to play with as a kid — though the Oronyx prophecies were always hit or miss.

**Phainon:** Yeah, all the kids would get so excited drawing cards like "Ruler" or "Warrior," but throw fits over "Devil" or "Drunkard" and demand a redo.

**Phainon:** If you just redo it when you don't like the result... what's the point of divination?

**Cyrene:** Ah, the blessing of childhood. Believing the gods might not notice our little corner of Aedes Elysiae, willing to let our small deceits slide...

**Cyrene:** Still... fate is absolute. After all, we never really know just how far into the future the prophecy extends.

**Phainon:** Anyway, let's go to our old "secret base" and look for those oracle cards—

**Phainon:** Um... now where did we hide those...

**Cyrene:** I can think of a few spots... Let's go check it out.

### Part 137 — `databank/missions/chapter-05-sun-set-to-die.md`
**Context:** Chapter 5 — For the Sun is Set to Die (v3.4) > Mission 3 — (Side) Hero, Return to That Peace of Home > Find Oracle Cards in the Sacrament Courtyard > Talk to Piso near the water, optional
**Kind:** collective-pointer
**Id:** `chapter-05-sun-set-to-die__find-oracle-cards-in-the-sacrament-courtyard-2`
**Collective:** `databank/memories/collective/chapter-05-sun-set-to-die.md`
**Witness:** spoke

**Livia:** Phainon, Cyrene, come quick! Look at this huge bottle I just caught!

**Piso:** Give it here! My rod, my catch. I only let you borrow it, remember?

**Phainon:** What's got you two so worked up? Here, show me...

*(Quick Read — Phainon's Childhood Drift Bottle: A handwritten letter sealed in the glass vial. On the wheatgrass-stitched paper, the handwriting blurs at the edges, softened by seeping water.)*

> *Dear straynger who finds this drift bottle in the future,*
>
> *It's currently the Month of Fortune, the final month of the year. Everyone in the village says if you do something tied to fortune on the last day of this month, you might be blessed with unexpeckted good luck. So, I decided to write this letter and cast it into the sea. Just let the currents decide where it goes. I want to share a little about our village... and my dream.*
>
> *Our little village is called Aedes Elysiae. Have you ever heard of it? Let me tell you a bit about it: We've got golden wheat fields that ripple like the sea, and tall, tall trees that shed their leaves. Right in the center of the village stands a statue of Oronyx. At the end of each year, the village gathers to celebrate the Winter Solstice. We leave offerings at the statue: roast graype pies, herb-fryed fish, goat's milk... all kinds of tasty food. Last year, I sneaked some malt candy and ended up with a tooth ripped straight out. Mom and Dad said if you throw a lost tooth under a tree, or bury it beneath a rock, a new one will grow back. Just like a seedling sprouting from the soil. I remember searching and searching, pushing through the tangled grass behind our courtyard, until I found this mysterious hole. I tossed the tooth inside, cupped my hands like a horn, and yelled, "Heeey — come ouuuut!" Nothing happened. When I went back this year... the whole was gone. Weird, right?*
>
> *You don't think I'm being childish, do you? I'm not like Cyrene. She can already host the festival, and she's great at telling the future with orecale cards. They say the card you draw shows what kind of person you'll become. Last time, I drew the "Deliverer." Everyone went "Woooow." People really like the Deliverer, but I'd rather be a Wonderer or a Skholar. Well, being a Deliverer sounds super amayzing, but it also means carrying a really, really heavy herden. I'm not that amazing, so I can't be the Deliverer.*
>
> *Few in Aedes Elysiae ever jerney far. We don't hear much about the outside world. All I know is there are all kinds of city-states out there and lots of great heroes. The bad part is, they're always at wars. I don't like wars. If the world were more like our village, where everyone knows each other and works together, harvesting grain, hearding sheep, why would we even need a Deliverer?*
>
> *But Mrs. Pythias and Uncle Galba both say that one day, when I'm a little older, I should go see the world for myself. If I could go anywhere, I'd visit the mighty Castrum Creamnose, find the greatest craftsman, and ask them to forje me a sword. With that sword, I'd protect our little village. I want no part in wars. Right now, I don't even have a sword of my own, just a stick and a hoe.*
>
> *Oh! And by then, I hope the outside world no longer needs a Deliverer. That would be the best thing ever.*
>
> *So that's my home, and that's my dream. What about you? What's your home like? What do you dream of? You could write it down, too. Toss it into the sea, and let the tides carry it to the next stop.*
>
> *Whoops! Just realized this letter has missspeelings, just like my essays in Mrs. Pythias's class. Please don't let her be the one who finds this, or I'm totally getting points docked and detenshun!*

**Phainon:** Wait a second... this is... This is my old Wish-In-A-Bottle! How did it end up here?

**Livia:** Ooh, I wanna see! What did little Phainon wish for?

**Phainon:** Not happening. This stays sealed.

**Female voice coming from afar:** Piso! Livia! Dinner's ready!

**Piso:** Coming, Mom!

**Livia:** Finally. I'm starving! See you later!

**Phainon:** You know what's funny? I really thought tossing that bottle into the sea would carry our wishes beyond Aedes Elysiae... maybe even bring something back to us...

**Phainon:** Never thought... the waves would end up pushing it back ashore.

> *(Trailblazer)* Funny how the past finds its way back.
> **Phainon:** Kind of disappointing, isn't it...
> *(Trailblazer)* I want to read it.
> **Phainon:** Don't bother... It's just some silly childish nonsense... Nothing worth reading.
> *(Trailblazer)* Seems more like a lake to me than an ocean.
> **Phainon:** Ah... well, that would explain it. What a shame.

---

*(If the player searched in the barn and near the water)*

**Cyrene:** No sign of the oracle cards...

**Cyrene:** Let's see... Ah! There's one place we haven't checked yet.

---

*(Enter the Sacrament Courtyard)*

**Phainon:** Those were the days. We'd always meet either by that statue in the village or right here.

**Cyrene:** Right! And I remember you never used the front door when you wanted me to come play. You always climbed over the wall. You were such mischief-makers!

**Cyrene:** Just like a certain someone who has a habit of showing up unexpectedly, right?

> *(Trailblazer)* Who's that supposed to be?
> **Cyrene:** Maybe it's you~♪
> *(Trailblazer)* Can't pin me down, can you?
> **Cyrene:** Ha! I bet (Trailblazer) said something clever just now.
> *(Trailblazer)* (Remain silent)
> **Cyrene:** Oh, giving me the silent treatment? How cruel!

**Phainon:** *ahem* Hey, I was just copying those storybook heroes. They never take the beaten path!

**Cyrene:** Still, it's embarrassing, I've been the only one here for a while, and I haven't kept up with the cleaning. With so many spots to check, where should we even start?

> *(Trailblazer)* Let's try the roof.
> **Cyrene:** Come to think of it, I haven't checked the roof in forever. Worth a shot, right?
> *(Trailblazer)* How about the courtyard?
> **Cyrene:** Leaves have taken over the courtyard...
> **Phainon:** I know, I know. We wouldn't want your clothes getting dirty. Leave the dirty work to me.
> **Cyrene:** That's not what I meant at all.
> *(Trailblazer)* What about the cellar?
> **Cyrene:** A cellar? I think I remember one... It was deep... so very deep...
> **Phainon:** Well, if we're talking wonderland stuff, it's not that different from my hero stories.
> **Phainon:** Alright, let's split up. I'll handle the yard, you check the upper floor.
