**The Hero Within:** And when hesitation took hold, the answer you sought from me...

**The Hero Within:** ...What am I saying? I am you. Even if you buried me deep and chose another path, that fact never changed.

**The Hero Within:** Then let's try again. When you stood before the scales in hesitation, the answer you found in your own heart...

**The Hero Within:** Do you still remember it?

**Phainon:** ...

**Phainon:** Remember the past, and become the hero of tomorrow.

**Phainon:** What weighs more than the fate of the world is the resolve to shoulder an immutable past, and carry it into the future.

**The Hero Within:** Good. Then, (Trailblazer), my friend, my comrade...

**The Hero Within:** You arrived in this strange world and were bound by its cruel fate up till this very moment.

**The Hero Within:** In the face of danger, you never once backed down. Instead, you've saved world after world, without hesitation. That boarding pass on your chest... is now the mark of a hero.

**The Hero Within:** If I choose to step through that door and embrace an end fitting of the Destruction, let this sun forged from millions and millions of Coreflames burn me up along with the god of Destruction and THEIR minions...

**The Hero Within:** Then the world we leave behind, this world, my home, where hope still lingers...

**The Hero Within:** Will you take on everything we've carried, return to the beginning one last time, stop the birth of Irontomb, save the world we love, and rewrite its doomed fate?

> *(Trailblazer)* I will.
> *(Trailblazer)* I will be the dawn of Amphoreus.
> *(Trailblazer)* I will... stop the completion of Irontomb.

**The Hero Within:** So...

**Phainon:** If (Trailblazer) takes on the weight of the whole world...

**Phainon:** (Trailblazer)'ll be able to walk further down the path of Deliverance than a fire that only knows how to burn.

**Phainon:** One will step into the future...

**Phainon:** One will remain in the past.

**Phainon:** If I can strike down the gods with Dawnmaker, perhaps fate's deadlock can be broken.

**Phainon:** And if you can stop Irontomb from ever being born... then these thirty million cycles will have not been in vain.

**Phainon:** It's time to put our teamwork to the test, partner.

> *(Trailblazer)* Go fight for the future, Phainon.
> *(Trailblazer)* Leave the world to me, Phainon.
> *(Trailblazer)* Let's both be the Deliverer, Phainon.

**(Trailblazer):** Thank you for all you've done already, friend.

**Phainon:** The image of the hero I've buried deep in my heart... finally steps into the light — here, at the end of this long Flame-Chase Journey...

**Phainon:** Cyrene. A page never seen before has just been turned.

**Phainon:** Lend me the power of Time and send this hero back to the origin of time, back to your side...

**Phainon:** And I will walk the unfinished path... just like the countless versions of me before, unwavering as always.

### Part 187 — `databank/missions/chapter-08-as-tomorrow.md`
**Context:** Chapter 8 — As Tomorrow Became Yesterday (v3.7) > Sub-Mission 6: Silver Chariot, Part With That Humanity's Epic > Phainon — "Let's Go on a Journey Together, Partner"
**Kind:** personal
**Id:** `chapter-08-as-tomorrow__phainon-let-s-go-on-a-journey-together-partner`

**Phainon:** You're here, partner. That celebration party I've been mentioning to you has finally become a reality. Though the vibe's a bit different from what I imagined... This still counts as keeping my promise, right?

> *(Trailblazer)* I thought... you had all faded away.

**Phainon:** What you speak of has indeed already come to pass. We triumphed over Irontomb, drew its fire unto ourselves, and purified its embers. Now, the force that maintains our consciousness and form... Comes from elsewhere. Don't worry. They'll be safe and sound. She promised.

> *(Trailblazer)* Cyrene...? / *Where is she...?*

**Phainon:** Don't worry. She always shows up at just the right moment. The Astral Express... honestly, it really feels like "home." Like how golden wheat stalks sway in my dreams every night through the countless cycles...

> *(Trailblazer)* Let's go on a journey together, partner.
> **Phainon:** Among all these stars in the sky, there must be another little village like Aedes Elysiae out there, right?
> *(Trailblazer)* There must be a village like that somewhere in the cosmos.
> **Phainon:** Are you inviting me to join your journey? I think I've found my own "wish," partner. Maybe... roaming the cosmos with you all is my strongest desire right now. The Xianzhou Alliance, Penacony... I blame my poor imagination. I can't picture what those worlds look like at all from Dan Heng's descriptions. So I have to see it with my own eyes, right?

> *(Trailblazer)* You'll see it with your own eyes. / *That's a promise, partner.*

**Mydei:** ...Something worth remembering? Nothing really left a deep impression.
**Mydei:** If I had to pick... then the straightforward style of pulling up vegetables and cooking them right away brings back memories of my days wandering in exile: simple, efficient, focused on sustenance over flavor.
**Phainon:** Funny isn't it? Mydei, a great Kremnoan warrior, is actually the most skilled cook among the Chrysos Heirs. No matter how simple the ingredients, they become a delicacy in his hands.
**Phainon:** The Kremnoan dictionary may be pretty sparse, but their recipe book? Vast and diverse.
**Mydei:** Want my advice? Forget that fancy salad. The mark of a great chef is making the most of what's at hand. They don't nitpick ingredients.
**Phainon:** Perhaps my cooking skills aren't on your level, but there's nothing wrong with making a salad. I stand by the ingredients I select and follow through with them. That's also what it means to be a proper chef.
**Mydei:** Hmph. At least you're practical. I can respect that.
**Phainon:** Back to the main point. The chief has been saying for ages how much they hope the fairies can enjoy a hearty, delicious feast.
**Phainon:** That's why seeing everyone's efforts to turn this dream into reality through our restaurant makes me so happy.
**Phainon:** As for regrets... Well, one would be that I couldn't best Mydei in the kitchen. The other is, I didn't get the chance to learn more specialty dishes before the restaurant's run came to an end. Time really does fly when you're having fun.
**Mydei:** During those years in exile, I often thought that... if only time could pass more slowly, they wouldn't have to leave me in such horrible ways.
**Mydei:** Still, reminiscing about the past doesn't change anything.
**Mydei:** The kindness you showed in taking care of my people has been repaid. And here's a piece of advice for you, Phainon of Aedes Elysiae.
**Phainon:** Speak your mind, Mydeimos. There's no need for restraint in this place.
**Mydei:** ...

> **Phainon:** ...
> **Phainon:** That said... I'm kind of drawing a blank.
> **Tribbie:** ...It is hard indeed.
> **Cerydra:** Heh. Since no one's willing to step up, allow me to take the reins.
> **Cerydra:** In honor and commemoration, I — leader of the Okhema Alliance, Imperator of the holy city — hereby propose the first-ever restaurant event.
> **Cerydra:** **The event theme shall be: "Imperator"!**
> **Cerydra:** During the event, all dishes must be prefixed with the noble title "Imperator," all staff shall refer to themselves as loyal subjects, and all customers must recite praises of the Imperator's glory before every meal.
> **Cerydra:** Thus, that earlier salad dish shall henceforth be renamed—
> **Cerydra:** **"Cery Salad."**
> *(Trailblazer)* ...That's a bit of a dry joke.

> **Tribbie:** **We** reject it too!

> **Cerydra:** ...

> **Cerydra:** Now that the spark of creativity has been lit, I'll leave you to your brainstorming.
>

> **Phainon:** When I think of Aedes Elysiae, the golden wheat fields come to mind. During the event, we could serve wheat-based dishes and decorate the place with bundles of wheat, symbolizing a bountiful harvest.
> **Cyrene:** But if the theme is "Aedes Elysiae," wouldn't that be a bit too difficult for the little fairies to understand?
> **Tribbie:** **We** have an idea! For the first event, it has to be something super appealing!
> **Tribbie:** Let's build the theme around the dish everyone loves! Can you guess?
> **Tribbie:** The answer is sweets! **We** know no one can resist the charm of desserts.
> **Tribbie:** The event theme will be — "Sweetie Festival!"
> **Cyrene:** Alright! Both proposals are on the table. Time to vote! One vote per person, so think carefully.
> **Cyrene:** Anyone in favor of Chef Phainon's proposal, raise your hand!
> **Phainon:** ...Me.
> *(Trailblazer)* (Raises hand)
>> **Cyrene:** That makes two votes for Phainon — his and (Trailblazer)'s.
> *(Trailblazer)* (Don't raise your hand)
>> **Cyrene:** Oh dear, only Phainon voted for himself.
>
> **Cyrene:** Now, those in favor of Lady Tribbie's proposal, raise your hand!
> **Tribbie:** **We** vote for it!
> *(Trailblazer)* (Raises hand)
> *(If the player agreed with Phainon earlier)* **Cyrene:** Manager... we said one vote per person. No abusing your power.
> *(If the player didn't agree with Phainon earlier)* **Cyrene:** So that's Tribbie, (Trailblazer), and me... three votes in total.
> *(Trailblazer)* (Don't raise your hand)
> *(If the player agreed with Phainon earlier)* **Cyrene:** Alright, guess it's just Tribbie and me — two votes in total.
> *(If the player didn't agree with Phainon earlier)* **Cyrene:** Fair enough. Abstaining counts too. So that's two votes for Tribbie's proposal.
>
> **Cyrene:** Which means the final tally is—
> **Tribbie:** Uh-uh, you miscounted. **We** (Tribbie, Trianne and Trinnon) all raised our hands! That's three votes! A landslide victory!
> **Phainon:** Huh!?
> **Phainon:** ...I yield. No one can win over Tribbie when it comes to voting.
> **Cyrene:** Then it's settled! The restaurant's first themed event will be "Sweetie Festival," and the event leads will be Tribbie, Trianne, and Trinnon.

**Phainon:** Great dishes start with great ingredients, and I believe in the wheat fields of Aedes Elysiae. I know exactly how flavorful this land's crops and meats are. Their unique taste is sure to leave a lasting impression.
**Mydei:** Well said. As for me, I'm going with "Hearty Vegetable Potage."
**Phainon:** Aren't you gonna tell us why?
**Mydei:** No need. Let's just get started.

**Castorice:** ...
**Judge Fairy:** Mem...
**Castorice:** Lord Phainon's dish may look plain at first glance, but one bite reveals its true depth: rich grain and oil-fried aromas, a crisp crust, and tender center. It's indeed a rare and delightful treat.
**Castorice:** Meanwhile, Lord Mydei has reimagined Aidonia's traditional stew with a touch of acidity that lifts the flavor. The broth is hearty without being heavy, and the bold northern spices have been mellowed into a warm, comforting finish.
**Castorice:** If this were a regular tasting, I'd give both dishes a perfect score.
**Castorice:** But... I get the feeling that's not the answer everyone's looking for.
**Team Phainon Fairy:** Memem! Mem! Keep cheering for Chef Phainon!
**Team Mydei Fairy:** Memem! Mem! Stop being so stubborn! You need to switch sides and vote for Mydei!
> *(Trailblazer)* Hard to tell who's gonna win.
> *(Trailblazer)* Still, every duel must have a winner...
> **Castorice:** So... what do we do now?
> **Phainon:** If these dishes are too evenly matched, let's just go another round. You don't mind, do you?
> **Mydei:** Not at all. It'd be rather boring if you were to lose the game too soon.
>
> **Phainon:** How about this one: "Double-Nut Mooncake"? Golden, crunchy, and packed with savory flavor. I bet Miss Castorice will love it.
> **Mydei:** Try my "Feast-Gravy Roast Chicken." It's juicy, full of flavor, and just the thing to balance out that overly salty taste of his.
> **Phainon:** ...
> **Mydei:** ...
>
> **Castorice:** Well... I'll have a quick taste. But you two, please stop cooking! No more new dishes!
> **Phainon:** Hey now, I haven't even brought out my signature dish! Presenting my "Cloudsheep Ribs." Judges, please enjoy.
> *(Trailblazer)* Wait, was that even on the menu?
> *(Trailblazer)* Where the heck did you even find a cloudsheep!?
>
> **Mydei:** Freshly made "Sweet & Sour Braised Chicken," coming right up.
> **Phainon:** We're not done yet. This "Salty Smoked Meat" is the pinnacle of my culinary career, my magnum opus that outperforms every other dish. You have to try it!
> **Mydei:** Here's some "Pickled Bean Purée." Enjoy.
> **Judge Fairy:** Mem... can't eat another bite, mem...
>
> **Castorice:** I motion to pause the tasting... What are we even supposed to do with all these extra dishes...
> **The Five Gourmet Overlords:** Mem! That's our cue! Our stomachs are like bottomless pits. We can handle anything, mem!
> **Phainon:** You done already? I'm just getting warmed up!
> **Mydei:** You think you've seen it all? Castrum Kremnos's cookbook is way thicker than you imagine. Let's keep going!
>
> **Phainon:** Alright then. "Salt-Seared Ham with Figs," dig in!
> **Mydei:** And here's "Roasted Hornswine with Lemon-Cream Sauce." Have a taste.
> **Phainon:** Try this one: "Surf & Turf Deluxe Stew." The name says it all.
> **Mydei:** "Honey-Crusted Fried Octopus!" Enjoy.
> **The Five Gourmet Overlords:** Mem... We surrender! Seriously, we're full to bursting!
> **The Five Gourmet Overlords:** Memem! Boss! Help us! Help the Maze, mem!
> **Hysilens:** Hmm? So I just need to eat every bait, right?
> **Hysilens:** ...Alright. I'm going in.
> *(The two chefs keep bringing out dish after dish, but somehow, the food is vanishing at an alarming rate...)*
>
> **Hysilens:** Delicious. This really is the ultimate feast. But... chefs, is there anything left you haven't served?
> **Phainon:** ...
> **Mydei:** ...
> **Phainon:** Haha... Isn't this something? We gave it everything we had, and still couldn't satisfy Miss Hysilens's appetite.
> **Mydei:** Seems the real winner of this culinary duel wasn't either of us.

**Announcer Fairy:** Memem... Mem! The Memchelin Food Review is about to begin. Please get ready, mem!
**Tribbie:** The fairies are really serious about this. Is it that formal of an event?
**Phainon:** Fairies hold food to the highest standards. As we all know, there's no matter too trivial in food quality.
**Announcer Fairy:** In the spirit of fairness, impartiality, and transparency, our judges will taste each dish on-site and deliver their final verdict, mem!
**Announcer Fairy:** Now then, mem! Please place your submitted dish on the designated table, mem!
**Cyrene:** We'll need something that best represents our restaurant. The choice of our signature dish now rests in your hands.
*(This is a huge moment. The food review can make or break a restaurant's reputation. This calls for a thorough deliberation. You picture the judges savoring the dish... And finally, the answer comes to your mind.)*
***(Trailblazer):*** The dish we're presenting is—
> *(Trailblazer)* Colorful Salad.
> *(Trailblazer)* Lettuce Salad.
>
> **Phainon:** Got it. I'll place the dish you chose onto the table.
> **Announcer Fairy:** Aside from the two traditional fairy judges, we've also invited a mystery guest for this review!
> **Announcer Fairy:** Word has it she's traveled far and wide, tasted countless delicacies, and is especially skilled at preparing raw ingredients. They say she's earned the title... "The Kitchen Slayer!"
> **Announcer Fairy:** What kind of twist will this special guest bring to the Memchelin Food Review? Stay tuned and find out, mem!
> **Phainon:** ...
> *(Trailblazer)* You're not getting nervous, are you?
> **Phainon:** Ha, haha... Of course not! I'm a professional chef now. A little food review's nothing! Totally under control!
> **Cyrene:** Who could this mysterious special guest be? The village chief never mentioned anything about this beforehand.
> *(Trailblazer)* I've got a feeling it's a familiar face.
> *(Trailblazer)* Shall we use the method of elimination?
> **Tribbie:** Ah! **We** see her! That new judge is—
>
> **Castorice:** Miss (Trailblazer), it's been a while. I didn't expect to meet you all here.
> **Phainon:** Miss Castorice? So, you're the special guest judge the fairies invited.
> **Tribbie:** Cas! You here to play with the little fairies too?
> **Castorice:** I'm a bit embarrassed to say it, but I actually applied to be the restaurant's chef. Unfortunately, my skills didn't pass the village chief's assessment.
> **Castorice:** Still, I do have a decent palate when it comes to gourmet dishes. So I accepted the fairies' invitation to act as a guest judge today.
> *(Trailblazer)* We're all friends here, maybe just let us pass?
>> **Castorice:** That... I'm afraid I can't. The results are determined by a vote between me and the two fairy judges. I can't decide it alone.
> *(Trailblazer)* Do you have the deciding vote?
>> **Castorice:** No... To keep things fair, the results will be jointly decided by me and two fairy judges.
>
> **Castorice:** Now that the dishes are in place, I'll help myself.
>
> **Castorice:** Hmm.
> **Phainon:** ...
> **Judge Fairy:** Mem! Memem, mememem!
> **Castorice:** I agree as well. Then, allow me to announce the outcome of this Food Review—
> **Castorice:** Your dishes were excellent.
> **Phainon:** Yes!
> **Castorice:** The freshness of the vegetables was immediately apparent, and the dressing added just the right depth of flavor.
